# Overlap Catalog

Source-refreshed catalog: PR states below belong to their dated audit, not a
live status feed. Recheck head/base, merge status and actual caller guards before
using a row. Frozen 2026-10-05 source changes and DSV4.1 guidance are maintained in
[source contracts](../../../docs/upstream-source-contracts.md) and
[DSV4.1 lessons](dsv41-kernel-optimization.md). Mainline availability is not proof
that a given deployment executes the path. A split trace is an eligibility or
dispatch hypothesis: verify shape, dtype, platform and actual caller guards
before concluding that a fusion is missing.

This catalog is the overlap-only companion to
`references/fuse-overlap-catalog.md`.

The scope includes fused kernels, collective/stream overlap, and GPU idle-gap / launch-overhead families. Host scheduling and metadata work belong here when a source-backed change removes GPU idle time; see the dedicated launch-overhead section.

Use it like this:

1. Start from the `overlap-opportunity table`.
2. Match visible kernel windows, collective windows, or stream-level overlap
   against the rows below.
3. If a match exists in the mainline sections, report it as an existing
   overlap family that is missing, disabled, regressed, or unsupported on the
   current backend.
4. If a match exists only in a status-audited PR section, report its explicit
   open, merged, or closed-unmerged state instead of calling it novel.
5. Only call an overlap opportunity "new" when no row in this file or
   `fuse-overlap-catalog.md` fits.

The `vLLM-origin` sections below are comparative references. They are not
necessarily present in the checked-out `sglang` tree, but they should still be
treated as upstream or analogous kernel-overlap families before labeling an
overlap opportunity as novel.

Refresh note `2026-10-05`: rechecked official main heads for SGLang
`b1bbd74f287f13ed1276b0403a01ebb55c597e93`, vLLM
`0c16eee3f1ff777298cc894c3eeb85f3880c6d6a`, TensorRT-LLM
`bb367fc8c1adf6e2c28c88cb1a8b46e1742a9d60`, and TokenSpeed
`6fa10840d5c3c23065f60428ad264fba60fa04ae`. Open SGLang `#21877`
and FlashInfer `#2720` remain status-sensitive references.
The public Kimi K3 Day-0 PR
[`sgl-project/sglang#32541`](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/32541)
merged on 2026-08-04 and shipped in v0.5.17. Its side-stream and PDL
rows are mainline provenance with shape/backend guards.
Closed-unmerged vLLM experiments are retained only when their implementation
idea remains useful and are labeled as such in the table.

FlashInfer comparison is pinned to `724e238e7deca2d6ffd5668d63ae95c428414f59` (0.7.1/main, 2026-10-04). SGLang and vLLM images pin 0.7.0.post1; `[0.7.1rc]` and `[main]` rows below are unavailable in those pins unless explicitly cherry-picked. Cake backends are experimental/opt-in unless the API allows experimental auto dispatch. Generic CuTe device names require custom-op/stack/caller attribution. Dates attached to merged commits are source commit dates unless explicitly API-audited.

## 1. LLM / SRT kernel-overlap families

| Pattern | Trace keywords | Primary code | Existing path | Skill should conclude |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Single-batch overlap (SBO) | MoE combine, down-gemm, shared-expert work in nearby two-stream windows | `python/sglang/srt/batch_overlap/single_batch_overlap.py` | combine vs down-gemm overlap, combine vs shared-expert overlap, one-stream dispatch+shared overlap, explicit SM partitioning and events | If exposed MoE combine sits near neighboring compute, classify it against SBO before calling it new overlap. |
| Q and K normalization on different streams | Q-side norm and K-side norm on different streams | `python/sglang/srt/models/utils.py::apply_qk_norm`<br>`python/sglang/srt/models/qwen3.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/models/qwen3_next.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/models/qwen3_5.py` | Q stays on current stream, K can run on `alt_stream` in capture mode | Treat split Q / K norm as an existing overlap family when `alt_stream` is already wired. |
| DeepSeek shared-expert / routed-expert overlap | shared-expert GEMMs near DeepEP dispatch / combine | `python/sglang/srt/models/deepseek_v2.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/batch_overlap/single_batch_overlap.py` | shared experts on `alt_stream`, overlap with dispatch / combine and down-gemm, Blackwell-specific env gating | This is an established routed-vs-shared branch overlap pattern, not a novel idea. |
| Llama4 shared branch vs routed branch overlap | shared expert branch plus routed MoE branch as adjacent windows | `python/sglang/srt/models/llama4.py` | shared expert on current stream, router + topk + routed experts on `alt_stream` | Use Llama4 as the first precedent for branch-level overlap in similar sparse models. |
| ExaoneMoE shared experts vs router experts overlap | shared expert output and router-expert output form a two-branch window | `python/sglang/srt/models/exaone_moe.py::forward_normal_dual_stream` | shared experts on current stream, router + routed experts on `alt_stream`, explicit join before combine | This is an existing dual-stream MoE overlap family. |
| Grok residual-MoE branch overlap | dense MLP and block-sparse MoE branches in parallel | `python/sglang/srt/models/grok.py::moe_with_rmoe` | dense MLP on current stream, MoE on `alt_stream`, fused dual residual RMSNorm around boundaries | Treat exposed Grok branch overlap as an existing pattern. |
| NSA dual-stream overlap | Q-proj, K-proj, RoPE, cache-store, quantization in tight two-stream windows | `python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/dsa/dsa_indexer.py` | Q / K projection split, RoPE split, cache-store vs quantization overlap | NSA already contains several dual-stream overlap precedents. |
| MoriEP async dispatch / combine comm stream | `MoriEP`<br>`_comm_stream`<br>`dispatch`<br>`combine`<br>`done_event` | `python/sglang/srt/layers/moe/token_dispatcher/moriep.py` | MoriEP can submit dispatch and combine onto a dedicated communication stream and synchronize only through events | Treat MoriEP comm / compute interleave as an existing MoE overlap family. |
| Generic `alt_stream` overlap families | `alt_stream` plus explicit `wait_stream` / `with torch.cuda.stream(...)` | `qwen2_moe.py`<br>`qwen3_moe.py`<br>`glm4_moe.py`<br>`bailing_moe.py`<br>`llada2.py`<br>`grok.py`<br>`olmo2.py`<br>`step3p5.py`<br>`longcat_flash.py`<br>`falcon_h1.py` | model-specific overlap on attention prep, MoE branches, or cache-store | Search these families before designing a new overlap scheme from scratch. |

| CUDA-graph stream explosion fixes | many stream rows in the trace<br>`torch.cuda.Stream` per replay | `python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/models/deepseek_v4_dspark.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/models/qwen4_exp.py` | Side streams created once and reused; DeepSeek-V2 dual-stream reordered main-first<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#40640 2026-09-21; #40658 2026-09-21; #39474 2026-09-15; earlier #30460 2026-07-09 | Hundreds of distinct stream rows in a K3/DSpark/Qwen3.8 trace indicate pre-fix builds, not real concurrency. |
| DeepSeek-V2/V3 dual-stream MoE (routed on main stream) + PCG dual-stream custom op | `dsv2_flashinfer_moe_dual_stream_graph`<br>`alt_stream` | `python/sglang/srt/models/deepseek_v2.py` | Routed experts stay on the main stream, shared experts on `alt_stream`; under piecewise CUDA graph a custom op keeps the fork (`SGLANG_ENABLE_PCG_DSV2_DUAL_STREAM`, default off)<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#29463 merged 2026-06-29; #30460 2026-07-09 | Corrects the overlap row: the shared branch is the side branch; under PCG the overlap is absent unless the env is set. |

NCCL ring all-gather for DSpark logits can mean no multicast plane. |

Two interleaved microbatches with DeepEP windows and smaller DeepGEMM grids are TBO by design. Rejected with DeepEP v2, MegaMoE, DSA index-topk sharing, HIP DSV4 prefill CP. |
| Non-EP DP-attention TBO comm stream | `dp_tbo_comm` stream<br>`dp_gather_partial_async`<br>`dp_reduce_scatterv_async`<br>`all_gatherv` / `reduce_scatterv` | `python/sglang/srt/layers/dp_attention.py::get_dp_tbo_comm_stream`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/dp_attention.py::dp_gather_partial_async` | Both microbatches' DP gather / reduce-scatterv run serially on one shared comm stream while the other microbatch computes<br>Evidence: #29362 merged 2026-07-05 (AMD DSV4 EP TBO prefill) | Gather/RS on a side stream next to attention/MoE compute in DSV4 DP TP-MoE TBO is this path; persistent per-(kind, subbatch) events avoid ROCm HSA event exhaustion. |
| Process-wide named side streams | `get_stream("alt")`, `get_stream("alt_mlp")`, `get_stream("dp_tbo_comm")`, `get_stream("offload")` | `python/sglang/srt/runtime_context.py::RuntimeContext.get_stream` | All models share one process "alt" stream lease instead of per-layer `torch.cuda.Stream()`<br>Evidence: #30348 merged 2026-07-07 | Two different overlap sites cannot run concurrently with each other on the same alt stream; overlap rows assume a single side stream unless the model allocates its own (DSV4, K3, DSpark). |

| DeepSeek MLA absorb-prepare overlaps | q/kv a-layernorm on `alt_stream`; `q_b_proj` vs DSA indexer during decode; opt-in born-FP8 Q8KV8 q-prep under the indexer | `python/sglang/srt/models/deepseek_common/attention_forward_methods/forward_mla.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/models/deepseek_common/attention_forward_methods/forward_mla_rocm.py` | Indexer chain and Q chain fork from the same `q_a_layernorm` output<br>Evidence: Present at SHA; Q8KV8 envs present (provenance not pinned) | Separate overlap windows for q-norm, q_b_proj and the indexer are existing; Q8KV8 overlap needs `SGLANG_ENABLE_DSA_Q8KV8_BORN_FP8_Q` + `SGLANG_ENABLE_DSA_Q8KV8_QPREP_OVERLAP`. |
| DeepSeek V2 MoE dual stream under piecewise / breakable graphs | `_can_dual_stream_graph`<br>shared experts on `alt_stream` beside trtllm-gen routed MoE | `python/sglang/srt/models/deepseek_v2.py` | Opt-in `SGLANG_ENABLE_PCG_DSV2_DUAL_STREAM`; only flashinfer_trtllm MoE, no fused shared experts, no A2A, no EPLB<br>Evidence: Present at SHA | Missing shared/routed overlap in a DSV2 piecewise/BCG trace is the default. |
| MegaMoE shared vs routed fork | `forward_mega_moe` fork on `moe.alt_stream`<br>`_should_overlap_shared_and_routed` (FlyDSL) | `python/sglang/srt/layers/moe/mega_moe.py::forward_mega_moe`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/moe/mega_moe_flydsl.py` | In capture mode, routed MegaMoE runs on the alt stream while unfused shared experts run on the main stream<br>Evidence: #39313 merged 2026-09-29; #35619 merged 2026-09-25 | With `SGLANG_OPT_DEEPGEMM_MEGA_MOE_FUSE_SHARED_EXPERTS` the shared branch disappears into the kernel; the fork remains for the routed branch only. |

Also check the frozen-head backend, shape, graph/capture and fallback guards before attributing the split to an old version. |
| Deferred FFN reduction carried into the next layer's fused AR + residual + norm (incl. attention DP), and per-model `--boundary-reduction` | `UnreducedOutput`<br>`all_reduce` vs `reduce_scatter` / `reduce_scatterv` at the FFN boundary<br>`FusedAddRMSNormKernel` / `allreduce_fusion` | `python/sglang/srt/layers/layer_boundary/output.py::UnreducedOutput`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/layer_boundary/fusions/allreduce.py::fused_attn_input`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/layer_boundary/residual/add_norm.py::flashinfer_ar_fusion_applies`<br>`python/sglang/srt/arg_groups/boundary_reduction.py` | The FFN all-reduce is not issued at layer exit. The partial sum is carried as `UnreducedOutput` and completed by the next layer's fused AR+residual+RMSNorm (FlashInfer/AITER/cutedsl), now also under attention DP (#41197). If the fused kernel declines a batch, a plain all-reduce completes it (#41193). `--boundary-reduction {auto,ar,rs,rsv,rs+rsv}` picks reduce-scatter variants per model (#41552)<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#41196 merged 2026-09-25 (`9d7f44bdbb`), #41197 merged 2026-09-25 (`963e9fb42d`), #41193 merged 2026-09-25 (`51c92e8df1`), #41552 merged 2026-09-28 (`49b5e76e45`). The rest of the 50-commit #41079..#41557 series is refactor/fix only | Layer-boundary traces show an all-reduce "missing" at the FFN exit and fused into the next layer's norm; that is not a lost collective. Rank-local partial sums at layer input are expected. Use `--boundary-reduction ar` to compare with plain AR. It does not disable AR fusion (use `--enforce-disable-flashinfer-allreduce-fusion`) |
| MegaMoE shared/routed fork on `alt_stream` (NVIDIA DeepGEMM and AMD FlyDSL) | `alt_stream`<br>`wait_stream`<br>mega-MoE region overlapped with shared expert or other graph work | `python/sglang/srt/layers/moe/mega_moe.py::forward_mega_moe`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/moe/mega_moe_flydsl.py::forward_mega_moe` | In capture mode the routed (or fused routed+shared) MegaMoE region runs on `alt_stream` and rejoins. AMD disables it under `SGLANG_AITER_MEGA_RANK_SYNC`<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#39313 merged 2026-09-29, sgl-project/sglang#35619 merged 2026-09-25 | If the MegaMoE region sits on the main stream with nothing overlapped, look for a lost fork (#39313 measured the regression) or rank-sync mode |
| FlashInfer MNNVL MoE all-to-all for prefill (replaces prefill all-gather + reduce-scatter) | `moeA2APrepareDispatchKernel`<br>`moeA2ADispatchKernel`<br>`moeA2APrepareCombineKernel`<br>`moeA2ACombineKernel` (now also in eager prefill)<br>disappearing NCCL all_gather / reduce_scatter around prefill MoE | `python/sglang/srt/layers/moe/token_dispatcher/flashinfer.py::FlashinferDispatcher`<br>`python/sglang/srt/arg_groups/moe_hook.py::validate_flashinfer_a2a_token_budget`<br>`python/sglang/srt/arg_groups/overrides.py::flashinfer_a2a_max_dispatch_tokens_per_rank`<br>Kernel/caller attribution: `cpp/tensorrt_llm/kernels/moe/communication/moeAlltoAllKernels.cu` | `--moe-a2a-backend flashinfer` now dispatches eager prefill through A2A. Per-rank workspace = `SGLANG_FLASHINFER_NUM_MAX_DISPATCH_TOKENS_PER_RANK`, or max(prefill buffer tokens, 4096) when unset. `--chunked-prefill-size > 0` is required. The StandardDispatcher AG+RS prefill fallback is removed.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#39818 merged 2026-10-04 (`a3275295c4`) | AG+RS around prefill MoE with the flashinfer A2A backend can mean a build before 2026-10-04. Body (GLM-5.2-NVFP4, 4x B300): +6.1% input tok/s at 4096 and 16384 prefill tokens/rank for trtllm_routed; within noise at <= 2048, which was already graph/A2A. |
| Interleave context-parallel transport (DP x TP x CP, EP-compatible) | `attn_cp_all_gather_into_tensor` / `attn_cp_reduce_scatter_tensor` (NCCL all_gather / reduce_scatter around attention)<br>CP padding masked before EP dispatch/combine all_to_all | `python/sglang/srt/layers/cp/interleave.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/cp/base.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/dp_attention.py::attn_cp_reduce_scatter_tensor`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/qwen_sparse_attn_backend.py::_forward_extend_cp` | Interleave transport is separated from boundary reduction (#42036), supports DP x TP x CP (#42038), and accepts scattered inputs with EP (#42041). Also: QSA prefill CP with zigzag query shards (#39721) and DSV4 HIP CP V2 (#34200, interleave order). The CUDA-graph CP batch alignment is skipped on HIP only (#40543).<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#42036 merged 2026-10-02; #42038 2026-10-04; #42041 2026-10-04; #39721 2026-10-01; #34200 2026-10-03; #40543 2026-10-03 | CP prefill traces show an all_gather before attention and a reduce_scatter after. This is a documented comm family, not a new overlap gap. Check `attn_cp_size` and the strategy (interleave vs zigzag). |
| Stage-boundary sum completion and FFN boundary-reduction policy | all-reduce vs fixed reduce-scatter vs variable reduce-scatter (`rsv`) at FFN exits<br>fused AR + residual + RMSNorm at the next layer's input<br>MoE deferred finalize folded into the CuteDSL MNNVL AR | `python/sglang/srt/layers/layer_boundary/exit.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/layer_boundary/stage.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/layer_boundary/fusions/allreduce.py::fused_ffn_input`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/layer_boundary/fusions/cutedsl.py::CuteDSLFusion`<br>`python/sglang/srt/arg_groups/boundary_reduction.py::model_boundary_reduction` | Compute never sums (`reduce_results=False` everywhere); the boundary completes the sum (#42301). The reduction-skip flags (`fuse_mlp_allreduce`, `skips_reduction`) are removed (#42312). This slice converts many more decoders to stage boundaries, so they now honor `--boundary-reduction`: #41779, #42304, #42307-#42311, #42477, #42478, #42481. Qwen4-Exp resolves to `ar` (#42478).<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#42301 and #42312 merged 2026-10-03; conversions merged 2026-09-30 to 2026-10-04. `--boundary-reduction` itself came from prior-slice #41552 (2026-09-28). | AR kernels now sit at layer boundaries, often fused with the next norm, not inside MLP/MoE. Reduce-scatter instead of AR under attention DP is the `rs`/`rsv` policy, not a bug. |
| MiniMax-M3 TP4 indexer context partitioning (ROCm, opt-in) | `_score_shard`<br>`_local_candidates`<br>`_merge_candidates`<br>all_gather of candidates (runtime or PyNCCL) | `python/sglang/kernels/ops/attention/minimax_sparse/decode/indexer_cp.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/minimax_sparse_ops/indexer_cp.py::make_indexer_cp` | The index-K cache stays replicated. Rank r scores blocks r, r+W, ..., takes local top-k candidates, then the ranks gather and merge exactly (score desc, block id asc). Decode only. `SGLANG_MINIMAX_M3_INDEXER_CP` defaults to False; the gather is warmed before graph capture.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#41488 merged 2026-10-02 (`b69a5f296a`) | Scoring work split across TP ranks plus a small all_gather in MiniMax-M3 decode is this opt-in path, not a new comm pattern. |

## 2. Staging / communication kernel-overlap families

| Pattern | Trace keywords | Primary code | Existing path | Skill should conclude |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Decode scatter on dedicated `scatter_stream` | `scatter_stream`<br>`_scatter_stream` | `python/sglang/srt/disaggregation/common/staging_handler.py` | staging scatter kernels are submitted to a dedicated stream so the decode thread does not block on the main forward stream | Treat decode-side staging scatter windows as an existing overlap pattern. |
| Staging-buffer fused gather / scatter kernels | `_fused_gather_to_staging_kernel`<br>`_fused_scatter_from_staging_kernel` | `python/sglang/srt/disaggregation/common/staging_buffer.py` | Triton kernels gather KV slices into contiguous staging memory and scatter them back to KV cache | If heterogeneous-TP staging shows many small copy kernels, compare against this existing fused-plus-overlap family first. |

The PR body also lists `SGLANG_PP_VLLM_ASYNC_RECV`, but it is **absent** at the frozen SHA (dropped before merge). |

Extend gathers the prefix for the next layer on a side stream while the current layer computes.<br>Evidence: `sgl-project/sglang#37615` merged 2026-09-16 (`e7f7447333`, "kv-shard 2/4") | **Merged but dormant:** `enable_kv_cache_sharding` has no declared parallel field at the frozen SHA. `test/registered/unit/test_runtime_context.py` lists it as undeclared: "nothing calls that path yet". Do not report it as a reachable optimization. |
| HiCache layer-wise load-back on a transfer stream, with TMA-staged host↔device kernel | `hicache_tma_transfer_kernel`<br>`hicache_transfer_per_layer`<br>`hicache_transfer_all_layer`<br>`hicache_transfer_per_layer_page_unified`<br>`LayerLoadingEvent`<br>`host_to_device_stream` / `device_to_host_stream` | `python/sglang/kernels/jit/csrc/kvcacheio/hicache_tma.cuh`<br>`python/sglang/kernels/ops/kvcache/hicache.py::use_hicache_tma_kernel`<br>`python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/l2_transfer.py::L2TransferEngine`<br>`python/sglang/srt/managers/cache_controller.py::LayerLoadingEvent` | Cached prefixes return layer by layer on a side stream while the forward runs, and the forward waits on per-layer events. The sm_90+ TMA kernel keeps a 192 KB `cp.async.bulk` ring per CTA and uses 4 CTAs (`TMA_BLOCK_QUOTA`); the register kernel needs 6+ blocks of 1024 threads to saturate the link.<br>Evidence: #40278 merged 2026-09-21 (`7ad55e4386`) | If the forward stalls on `LayerLoadingEvent` waits, the host link is the critical path. Check the `SGLANG_HICACHE_TMA_TRANSFER` default, row size (multiple of 16 B), page tiling, and HIP (no TMA). The PR measured GB300 at H2D 97→192 GB/s and D2H 93→183 GB/s using 4 of 152 SMs. |

Topology-aware configs, NVLS algorithms when NVLS + symmetric memory exist (e.g. GB200), and multi-IPC-domain topologies. NCCL fallback per message size<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#37442 merged 2026-09-24 (`e047e50d4e`). Base MSCCL++ AR since #6277 (2025-06-05) | Non-NCCL collective kernels on GB200/multi-node with `--enable-mscclpp` are this family. Compare their message-size coverage before calling NCCL comm time irreducible |
| SM120 FlashInfer PCIe-IPC all-reduce for switch-free hosts | `ipc_tp2_remote_push_kernel`<br>`ipc_rsag_ring_push_param_kernel`<br>`ipc_topo_rsag8_push_param_kernel`<br>`push_oneshot_param_kernel`<br>`ce_publish_flag_kernel` (FlashInfer `include/flashinfer/comm/pcie_ipc_*.cuh`) | `python/sglang/srt/distributed/device_communicators/pcie_ipc_ar.py::PcieIpcCommunicator`<br>`python/sglang/srt/distributed/parallel_state.py`<br>Kernel/caller attribution: `include/flashinfer/comm/pcie_ipc_all_reduce.cuh`<br>`include/flashinfer/comm/pcie_ipc_ce_ring.cuh` | `SGLANG_ENABLE_PCIE_IPC_ALLREDUCE=1` (default off) on SM120 without NVLink. Staged pushes keep one in/out stream per rank, with a 4+4 island decomposition at 8 ranks. The workspace is decode-sized (`max_bs*draft_tokens*hidden`; override `SGLANG_PCIE_IPC_MAX_NUMEL`), so full prefill chunks stay on NCCL<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#34528 merged 2026-09-28 (`c7be3e935b`). 8xSM120 hidden 6144: 12 KiB 5.3 us vs NCCL 205 us; TP8 8k TPOT 21.14->13.62 ms, 35.02->48.05 tok/s | On RTX PRO 6000 / 5090-class multi-GPU hosts, NCCL ring kernels at decode sizes are the gap this closes. Expect NCCL still on prefill by design |
| HiCache page-unified KV load-back JIT kernel (H2D from page-unified host layout) | `hicache_transfer_per_layer_page_unified` | `python/sglang/kernels/jit/csrc/kvcacheio/hicache.cuh`<br>`python/sglang/kernels/ops/kvcache/hicache.py::transfer_hicache_one_layer_page_unified_lf` | Per-layer load-back reads `(head_group, layer, K/V, page, head, dim)` host pages in place, with 16 B/thread coalesced over PCIe, for MHA and MLA. **Not wired**: no srt caller at the frozen SHA<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#39726 merged 2026-09-28 (`e581520c67`) | Not yet a dispatch path. Do not flag its absence in HiCache traces |

## 3. VLM / diffusion kernel-overlap families

| Pattern | Trace keywords | Primary code | Existing path | Skill should conclude |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Vision QK norm with aux stream | vision-side QK norm or norm-like kernels before attention | `python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/vision.py` | vision QK normalization can call shared `apply_qk_norm(...)`, with K-side work on `aux_stream` | If vision QK prep is split, first check this existing aux-stream path. |
| ViT CUDA graph disables vision aux stream | expected vision overlap is absent under ViT graph | `python/sglang/srt/models/internvl.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/vision.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/environ.py::SGLANG_VIT_ENABLE_CUDA_GRAPH` | vision `aux_stream` is intentionally disabled when ViT CUDA graph is on | Missing vision overlap may be intentional, not a regression. |
| Ulysses sequence-parallel attention | exposed `all_to_all` around attention blocks | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/layers/attention/layer.py`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/distributed/communication_op.py` | head / sequence redistribution before and after attention | Treat sequence-parallel all-to-all as an existing distributed attention family. |
| USP attention with all-to-all and ring attention | `all_to_all`, ring-attention comm, head / sequence reshards | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/layers/attention/layer.py` | `_usp_input_all_to_all(...)`, `_usp_output_all_to_all(...)`, `ring_attn(...)` | This is the primary existing overlap / comm family for many diffusion models. |
| Turbo-layer async all-to-all pipelining | pipelined A2A windows with explicit waits on a comm stream | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/layers/attention/turbo_layer.py` | looped `all_to_all_single(..., async_op=True)` plus staged postprocess on a comm stream | Treat exposed turbo A2A windows as an existing pipelined overlap pattern. |
| TorchInductor compute / communication reorder | compiled traces with compute and comm partially interleaved | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/pipelines_core/stages/denoising.py`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/pipelines_core/stages/model_specific_stages/mova.py` | `torch._inductor.config.reorder_for_compute_comm_overlap = True` | Existing compile-time reordering may already explain partial overlap in diffusion traces. |

| Pattern | Trace keywords | Primary code | Existing path | Skill should conclude |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Merged PR `#32541` Kimi K3 branch and PDL overlap | `bfa_alt_stream`<br>`gate_alt_stream`<br>`shared_event`<br>`set_mla_kv_concat_q`<br>`enable_pdl` | [`sgl-project/sglang#32541`](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/32541)<br>`python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py`<br>`python/sglang/kernels/ops/attention/set_mla_kv_concat_q.py` | Public Day-0 branch overlaps independent KDA, MLA-gate, and shared-expert branches, and uses a fused MLA prologue as a PDL producer for attention; merged 2026-08-04. | Treat these as mainline, shape-gated upstream patterns; first measure available slack, confirm joins and tensor lifetimes, and verify the dispatcher did not select a serialized fallback. |
| Merged PR `#32541` skinny KDA branch vs wide QKVG projection | small `f_a/b` GEMVs near a wide `qkvg` GEMM<br>`bfa_alt_stream` | [`sgl-project/sglang#32541`](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/32541)<br>`python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py::forward_qkvbfg_fused` | The small dependent GEMV chain runs on an auxiliary stream while the wide sibling projection remains on the caller stream; both read the same hidden state and join before beta/forget-gate consumers; merged 2026-08-04. | Apply only when both branches are independent, the small branch fits inside the wide GEMM's slack, and capture mode can represent the fixed stream/join topology. |
| Merged PR `#32541` MLA output-gate projection vs attention core | `gate_alt_stream`<br>`_fork_output_gate`<br>`mla_output_gate` | [`sgl-project/sglang#32541`](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/32541)<br>`python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py`<br>`python/sglang/kernels/ops/attention/mla_output_gate.py` | The gate projection is issued on an auxiliary stream and kept alive until the attention output reaches the gated output projection; merged 2026-08-04. | Retain the lazy path when graph segmentation or batch size makes the join unsafe or slower; a serialized gate GEMM is not automatically a regression. |
| Merged PR `#32541` shared experts vs routed dispatch / A2A | shared-expert GEMMs beside routed-expert dispatch, A2A, or latent tail<br>`shared_event` | [`sgl-project/sglang#32541`](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/32541)<br>`python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py` | Shared experts are issued after the MoE front on a side stream so their shorter branch hides under routed communication and latent work, then rejoins as late as correctness allows; merged 2026-08-04. | Starting the side branch earlier can steal bandwidth from the critical front; choose placement from the trace and record tensor lifetime on the consuming stream. |
| Merged PR `#32541` ReplaySSM verify/fold staging | `replayssm_rawv`<br>`replayssm_rawk`<br>`accept_lens`<br>`kda_replayssm_exact_fold_kernel` | [`sgl-project/sglang#32541`](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/32541)<br>`python/sglang/kernels/ops/attention/fla/kda_replayssm_spec_decode.py`<br>`python/sglang/kernels/ops/attention/kda_decode_mtp.py` | Verify stores compact raw inputs without mutating the committed state; one post-sampling batched fold advances all accepted layer/head states; merged 2026-08-04. | Classify this primarily as dependency and launch elimination, not concurrent-stream overlap; do not schedule the fold before accept lengths are final. |
| Merged PR `#32541` MLA prologue producer-to-attention PDL chain | `set_mla_kv_concat_q`<br>`set_mla_kv_concat_q_fp8`<br>`cudaGridDependencySynchronize`<br>`enable_pdl` | [`sgl-project/sglang#32541`](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/32541)<br>`python/sglang/kernels/ops/attention/set_mla_kv_concat_q.py`<br>`python/sglang/kernels/jit/csrc/elementwise/` | A fused prologue publishes KV scatter and concatenated/quantized Q data, then permits the dependent attention launch to begin without waiting for the producer grid to retire completely; merged 2026-08-04. | Treat this as same-stream dependency overlap, not arbitrary launch reordering; producer visibility and trigger placement must be verified in source and with race-sensitive tests. |
| Open PR `#21877` fused down-GEMM + combine superseding SBO | `enable_fused_grouped_gemm_combine`<br>`combine`<br>`down_gemm` | `PR #21877`<br>`python/sglang/srt/server_args.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/moe/token_dispatcher/deepep.py` | Fused combine eliminates the standalone combine window, so SBO is intentionally disabled when this path is on; OPEN, stale/conflicting (updated 2026-06-10); enable_fused_grouped_gemm_combine is absent from frozen main. | PR-branch candidate, not executable via this flag on frozen main. Check current fused backends and dispatcher before proposing or assuming this overlap. |

## 5. FlashInfer kernel-overlap families

These rows are comparative references from `flashinfer`. Use them when a trace
looks like an upstream FlashInfer overlap family even if the current `sglang`
checkout only calls part of that implementation.

| Pattern | Trace keywords | Primary code | Existing path | Skill should conclude |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| FlashInfer PDL launch-overlap family | `enable_pdl`<br>`launch_with_pdl`<br>`cudaGridDependencySynchronize`<br>`cudaTriggerProgrammaticLaunchCompletion`<br>`trigger_completion_at_end=False`<br>`allreduce_fusion` | `flashinfer/norm/__init__.py`<br>`flashinfer/activation.py`<br>`flashinfer/rope.py`<br>`flashinfer/comm/allreduce.py`<br>`flashinfer/comm/trtllm_ar.py` | FlashInfer uses Programmatic Dependent Launch broadly, and the allreduce path can further advance completion so the next PDL-aware kernel overlaps on the same stream | Treat tight same-stream dependent windows and allreduce-followed-by-kernel windows as one existing FlashInfer launch-overlap family first. |
| FlashInfer CuTeDSL MoE aux-stream async-memset overlap | `aux_stream`<br>`main_event`<br>`memset_event`<br>`use_async_memset` | `flashinfer/fused_moe/cute_dsl/fused_moe.py` | Preallocated MoE output is zeroed on an auxiliary CUDA stream while GEMM1 runs on the main stream, then both streams join before finalize; requires use_async_memset AND use_fused_finalize. SGLang fused finalize defaults False; main-only #5319 (2026-10-01) moves unfused-path overlap to PDL. | Missing aux-stream memset can be intentional when fused finalize is off; consumer pins do not include main-only #5319. |
| FlashInfer green-context SM partition overlap | `split_device_green_ctx`<br>`split_device_green_ctx_by_sm_count`<br>`green_ctx` | `flashinfer/green_ctx.py` | CUDA green contexts partition SMs and create dedicated streams for concurrent kernel families on separate SM slices | Treat SM-partitioned concurrency as an existing FlashInfer overlap mechanism, not a novel scheduler idea. |

FlashInfer AG+GEMM exists but has no SGLang/vLLM caller. |

The fused variant folds the merge in (see A).<br>Evidence: #2951 2026-04-23; #5172 2026-09-14; #4929 2026-09-21; #5512 2026-09-23 [0.7.1rc]; #5948 [main] | Compare with the vLLM-origin DCP async A2A row. The copy-engine variant moves data with CE memcpy and SM flag kernels, freeing SMs for overlap. AG/RS reported 2.81x geomean over NCCL graphs at 1–32 tokens.<br>Evidence: #4393 2026-08-20 [0.7.0]; #4870 2026-09-10, #5169 2026-09-13, #5023 2026-09-29 [0.7.1rc] | On PCIe-only boxes, an NCCL all-reduce hotspot may be replaced by these kernels. Tiny flag kernels plus memcpy rows in the trace are the CE variant, not idle time. SGLang: `python/sglang/srt/distributed/device_communicators/pcie_ipc_ar.py` (sgl-project/sglang#34528 2026-09-29). vLLM: `vllm/distributed/device_communicators/flashinfer_pcie_ipc_all_reduce.py` (vllm-project/vllm#53576 2026-09-01). |
| Mixed comm (fused AR+AG and RS+AR kernels) | `fused_allreduce_allgather_kernel`<br>`fused_reducescatter_allreduce_kernel` | `flashinfer/comm/mixed_comm.py`<br>`include/flashinfer/comm/mixed_comm.cuh` | Single kernels for hybrid TP/DP collective combinations.<br>Evidence: #2563 merged 2026-04-24 (pre-window) [0.7.0] | Two back-to-back collectives in a hybrid-parallel trace may be fusable here. No consumer. |
| PDL extensions | `launchWithPdlWhenEnabled`<br>`griddepcontrol_wait`<br>`griddepcontrol_launch_dependents`<br>`PDL_EARLY` | `flashinfer/fused_moe/core.py`<br>`flashinfer/fused_moe/cute_dsl/fused_moe.py`<br>`flashinfer/fused_moe/cute_dsl/blackwell/blockscaled_contiguous_grouped_gemm_finalize_fusion.py` | PDL now covers the intermediate per-token NVFP4 quant between TRT-LLM MoE FC1 and FC2 (#5281), CuTe-DSL MoE routing (`moe_sort`, #5497), the Kimi-K3 vision tower (`PDL_EARLY` prologue, #5691) and the host-decided trigger in Cake sampling (#5636). On the unfused-finalize CuTe-DSL path (`enable_pdl and not use_fused_finalize`), the FC2 kernel calls `griddepcontrol_launch_dependents()` early so that the standalone finalizer prologue overlaps FC2 (#5319). This replaces the aux-stream memset overlap on that path.<br>Evidence: #5281 2026-09-17; #5497, #5682 2026-09-29; #5636, #5691 [0.7.1rc] | Tight FC1→quant→FC2 and routing→GEMM1 windows are expected only when `enable_pdl` reaches these launches. On ≤0.7.0.post1 the routing launch ignored `enable_pdl` (fixed in #5497). |

PP sampling uses multiple streams (default `1`)<br>Evidence: `#15727` 2026-09-02; `#18134` 2026-08-28; `#18694` 2026-09-04 | KV transfer kernels or copies overlapping context compute are expected in disaggregated prefill traces. |
| Generic two/N-stream helpers (TRT-LLM style) | `maybe_execute_in_parallel`<br>`execute_in_parallel`<br>`aux_stream()`<br>`lora_linear_async` | `vllm/utils/multi_stream_utils.py`<br>`vllm/utils/torch_utils.py::aux_stream`<br>`vllm/models/kimi_k3/nvidia/low_latency_gemm.py`<br>`vllm/models/kimi_k3/nvidia/mla.py`<br>`vllm/models/deepseek_v41/attention.py`<br>`vllm/lora/layers/base_linear.py` | Users: Kimi-K3 KDA projections (QKVG ∥ F_A/beta→F_B, TP8, M≤16, #54697); Kimi-K3 MLA `g_proj` side stream for M<512 (#54015); Kimi-K3 latent-MoE shared experts; DSV4/V4.1 attention with 3 aux streams (wqa/wkv ∥ indexer/kv-score, `VLLM_MULTI_STREAM_GEMM_TOKEN_THRESHOLD`=1024); Inkling qkvr prep/MoE; TritonExperts; LoRA base ∥ adapter (`VLLM_LORA_ENABLE_DUAL_STREAM`, off by default).<br>Evidence: #54697 merged 2026-09-01; #54015 merged 2026-08-28; #41526 merged 2026-05-02; #57407 merged 2026-09-27 | Side-stream windows in these models are upstream overlap families. `maybe_execute_in_parallel` silently serializes while a breakable CUDA graph is capturing. `execute_in_parallel` runs serially unless the caller passes `enable=True` (the ROCm platform gate). Large-M fallback above the token thresholds is by design. |
| TRT-LLM PyTorch-backend aux-stream branch overlap (`maybe_execute_in_parallel`), here for the Kimi K3 KDA QKVG vs beta/forget-gate projections at ≤128 rows | `maybe_execute_in_parallel`<br>`_projection_aux_stream`<br>KDA `qkvg` GEMM overlapped with the `beta`/`g`/`onorm_lowrank` projections | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/modules/multi_stream_utils.py::maybe_execute_in_parallel`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/modules/kimi_kda/kimi_kda_mixer.py` (`_KDA_BFA_MULTISTREAM_MAX_ROWS = 128`) | Existed before the slice. #17822 consolidated it into the KDA mixer. The row cap is SGLang's Blackwell cutoff and untuned for TRT-LLM (comment in source). For verify it counts `B * num_steps` rows.<br>Evidence: `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#17822` merged 2026-08-24 (`88592cda9d`, consolidation); `#18728` (2026-09-09) later shared Kimi aux streams | A KDA projection pair on one stream with 128 rows or fewer can mean multi-stream is disabled (`do_multi_stream()`) or no aux stream was passed. Above 128 rows serialization is intended. This generic PyTorch-backend helper replaces the deleted AutoDeploy multi-stream rows (see C). |
| TRT-LLM Helix decode context parallelism for MLA (per-rank partial O + softmax stats, all-to-all, LSE combine), wired for Kimi K3 (KDA-TP + MLA-DCP + MoE-EP) and enabled on CuTe DSL MLA | `trtllm::alltoall_helix` / `alltoall_helix_native`<br>`trtllm::helix_post_process` / `helix_post_process_native`<br>CuTe DSL MLA decode emitting `softmax_stats` | `cpp/tensorrt_llm/thop/alltoallOp.cpp`<br>`cpp/tensorrt_llm/thop/helixPostProcessOp.cpp`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/attention/backends/fmha/cute_dsl_mla.py::CuteDslMlaFmha`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/models/modeling_kimi_linear.py` | #17796 runs K3 KDA layers TP and MLA layers decode-CP on a `tp=1 × cp=N` server. Attention-DP is off and spec decode is off in phase 1. Context warmup is floored to `cp_size*tokens_per_block+1`. #18131 lets `CuteDslMlaFmha` write `[B*S_q, H, 2]` fp32 softmax stats for the helix combine (single-token at the PR, multi-token at the frozen SHA).<br>Evidence: `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#17796` merged 2026-08-25 (`f1f9f00b08`)<br>`#18131` merged 2026-08-28 (`ec411dcedb`) | An a2a + `helix_post_process` pair after each MLA decode is the expected helix comm cost. It is a DCP family, not a missing fusion. Compare against vLLM `dcp_alltoall` (overlap-catalog vLLM DCP row). |
| TRT-LLM VisualGen VSA + Ulysses no longer forces `NCCL_NVLS_ENABLE=0` | Ulysses all-to-all / NCCL collectives may use NVLS (NVLink SHARP) kernels again | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/visual_gen/executor.py` | Earlier builds set `NCCL_NVLS_ENABLE=0` for CuTeDSL VSA + Ulysses to dodge a Blackwell hang. #16751 removes the override.<br>Evidence: `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#16751` merged 2026-08-25 (`a1b3eb6489`) | Collective kernel names and bandwidth differ between pre- and post-#16751 VisualGen multi-GPU traces. Do not attribute the difference to the attention change. |

MLA uses the shared registered custom op. The old padded-batch assertion was removed. Enabled with `prefill_cuda_graph_backend='breakable'` (default `disabled`).<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#17816 merged 2026-09-04 | K3 prefill traces with graph launches between eager KDA/MLA blocks are the BCG path. Compare SGLang BCG and TokenSpeed breakable prefill graphs. |
| TRT-LLM Kimi K3 shared auxiliary streams (role to stream mapping) | Up to 5 streams in K3 traces: main + {Attention=MoeShared}, MoeChunkingOverlap, MoeBalancer, MoeOutputMemset | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/models/modeling_kimi_linear.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/utils.py::AuxStreamType`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/modules/multi_stream_utils.py::maybe_execute_in_parallel` | Attention side work (forget-gate projection chain vs qkvg) and MoE shared-expert overlap share one stream, because those phases are sequential. | TRT-LLM DWDP (Distributed Weight Data Parallelism): MoE expert-weight P2P prefetch on a dedicated copy stream, two layers ahead | `cudaMemcpyBatchAsync` / P2P memcpy on a side copy stream<br>`prefetch_events` / `consume_events` (`wait_event`) | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/modules/dwdp/weight_manager.py::DWDPWeightManager`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/dwdp.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/modules/dwdp/setup.py` | The compute stream waits on the prefetch event for slot i while the copy stream waits on the consume event before overwriting it. Weights are bound through a composite VA (CUDA VMM plus MNNVL fabric memory on GB200). The CPU never synchronizes. Enabled via `dwdp_config` in llm_args.<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18644 merged 2026-09-10 (lifecycle hardening). The family originates in #12136 (2026-04-02) and #14453 (2026-06-01), both pre-slice. | Long P2P memcpys on a side stream in a DWDP MoE trace are intended overlap. Only report them when the compute stream waits on `prefetch_events`, which can mean the copy is exposed. |
| TRT-LLM disaggregated KV transfer through the C++ NIXL bounce pipeline (gather into a bounce arena on copy streams, credit-scheduled chunks) | `batchedCopyKernel` (gather/scatter) on `copy_stream_count` streams<br>NVTX domain `trtllm.disagg.bounce`<br>threads `bounceIO` / `bounceScatter` | `cpp/tensorrt_llm/executor/cache_transmission/nixl_utils/bounce/GatherScatterKernel.cu`<br>`cpp/tensorrt_llm/executor/cache_transmission/nixl_utils/bounce/BounceTransport.cpp`<br>`cpp/tensorrt_llm/executor/cache_transmission/nixl_utils/bounce/BounceNvtx.h`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/disaggregation/nixl/bounce_knobs.py` | KV blocks are gathered into a shared bounce buffer (`kv_cache_bounce_size_mb`) and moved in chunks under a credit scheduler with eager gather. They are then scattered at the receiver, so transfer overlaps with compute. Enable with `CacheTransceiverConfig.agent_bounce_buffer_enable`; tune with `agent_bounce_params` or `TRTLLM_NIXL_BOUNCE_*`. NVTX is compiled out unless built with `-DNVTX_DISABLE=OFF`.<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#15780 merged 2026-09-16 | In disaggregated nsys traces, `batchedCopyKernel` on side streams on the context or generation worker is the bounce pipeline. Many small NIXL descriptors without it can mean the bounce path is off. |
| TRT-LLM KVCacheManagerV2 host-tier batched page copy (offload/onboard sized to run alongside inference), plus background-thread resize | `largePageCopyKernel` / `smallPageCopyKernel` / `genericPageCopyKernel`<br>`copyPageIndicesKernel`<br>`cuMemcpyBatchAsync` (PCIe hosts) | `cpp/tensorrt_llm/batch_manager/kv_cache_manager_v2/batchedPageCopy.cu`<br>`cpp/tensorrt_llm/batch_manager/kv_cache_manager_v2/batchedPageCopy.h` | `BatchedPageCopier` picks an LDGSTS kernel on coherent NVLink-C2C or `cuMemcpyBatchAsync` on PCIe. Resize, create and resume can run on a background thread under a reader-writer lock.<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18541 merged 2026-09-11 | Low-occupancy page-copy kernels running alongside decode on GH200/GB200 are deliberate KV offload/onboard. Long engine-thread stalls during a burst resize to very long sequences point to the pre-#18541 inline path. |
| MiniMax-M3 piecewise CUDA graph covers QKV projection + norm + RoPE + FP8 KV/index-K insert. Overlapping prologues are expected. |

| Kimi K3 MoE: the shared-expert output all-reduce moves onto the aux stream to overlap the routed expert chain. A single concat+AR is the pre-#19182 layout. |

| Speculative capture buffers (DFlash/DSpark hidden-state taps) shared across CUDA-graph buckets | (memory, not kernels) one `[max_num_tokens, hidden*layers]` buffer instead of one per bucket | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/speculative/dflash.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/speculative/dspark.py` | Always on. Forwards and consumers run on one stream.<br>Evidence: `#19350` (`0d52c6d2`) merged 2026-09-21 | Pre-#19350 builds lose KV capacity per captured bucket with DFlash/DSpark. Compare capacity only within the same build. Also check the frozen-head backend, shape, graph/capture and fallback guards before attributing the split to an old version. |

## 8. TokenSpeed-origin kernel-overlap families

These rows are comparative references from `lightseekorg/tokenspeed`. Use them
when the trace is from TokenSpeed or from a vLLM/TokenSpeed hybrid deployment.

| Pattern | Trace keywords | Primary code | Existing path | Skill should conclude |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

With `AttentionConfig.hisparse_config` plus `HiSparseConnector` (`host_pool_gib`), decode top-k rows are resolved against a GPU hot buffer (LRU). The combine kernel was ~17% faster on decode-sized calls but slower on large prefill calls, and +4.0% throughput / −2.7% median ITL at DEP8 (DSR1 NVFP4, GB300). Check the env before calling combine bandwidth a gap. |
| vLLM-origin MoE-EP megakernel **passthrough all2all** (communication folded into the expert kernel) | `all2all_backend="passthrough"`<br>`PassThroughAll2AllManager`<br>no dispatch/combine kernels | `vllm/distributed/device_communicators/all2all.py::PassThroughAll2AllManager`<br>`vllm/model_executor/layers/fused_moe/all2all_utils.py`<br>`vllm/config/kernel.py::bind_passthrough_all2all_backend` | Bound automatically for `flashinfer_moe_ep_*`. It cannot be selected from the CLI.<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#54049 merged 2026-10-02 (`111f71a6`) | The absence of all2all windows is expected with MegaMoE, so the overlap analysis must look inside the megakernel and not at stream gaps. |
| vLLM-origin DeepEP v2 NVLink+RDMA **hybrid mode auto-selected by default** | `VLLM_DEEPEP_V2_ALLOW_HYBRID_MODE`<br>`railedGinType` vs `ginType` | `vllm/distributed/device_communicators/all2all.py::DeepEPV2All2AllManager`<br>`vllm/utils/nccl.py::query_nccl_gin_type`<br>`vllm/envs.py` | The default flipped 0→1. DeepEP now chooses hybrid (multi-node RDMA) vs NVLink itself, using the railed GIN type query.<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#57991 merged 2026-09-29 (`50239dfc`) | Multi-node DeepEPv2 traces may now show two-tier RDMA+NVLink dispatch/combine by default. Set the env to 0 to reproduce older traces. |
| vLLM-origin PCP **decode-request sharding** (PCP-only: each decode request has one PCP owner, and the others all-gather its KV) | `pcp_shard_decode_requests`<br>PCP KV all-gather of decode KV<br>zero-token placeholder rows | `vllm/config/parallel.py::pcp_shard_decode_requests`<br>`vllm/v1/attention/ops/pcp.py`<br>`vllm/v1/worker/gpu/pcp_manager.py`<br>`vllm/v1/attention/backends/mla/indexer.py` | Active when `prefill_context_parallel_size>1` and DCP==1. DCP keeps the replicated decode path.<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#52162 merged 2026-10-01 (`8486f1eb`) | Decode compute is no longer replicated across PCP ranks. Expect a per-rank decode batch of about 1/PCP plus an extra KV gather. The PR measures +2% to +6% req/s (4×H20 PCP4+EP4, 90–99% prefix-hit) and one −2.16% boundary point. |
| vLLM-origin PP: skip sampled-token broadcasts for requests that finish at prefill | `compute_need_sampled_mask`<br>PP sampled-token broadcast | `vllm/v1/worker/gpu/pp_utils.py`<br>`vllm/v1/worker/gpu/input_batch.py` | MRV2 with PP>1 and prefill in the batch.<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#58542 merged 2026-09-30 (`7314360c`) | Fewer PP broadcast collectives per step. This is minor and only visible as fewer small NCCL ops. |
| vLLM-origin async-EPLB contention-aware expert migration batching | record_function `eplb: schedule migration batches`<br>batched P2P expert-weight transfers | `vllm/distributed/eplb/migration_scheduler.py`<br>`vllm/distributed/eplb/rebalance_execute.py`<br>`vllm/distributed/eplb/async_worker.py` | `EPLBConfig.enable_migration_batching` (default off, async EPLB only). Each rank talks to at most one peer per batch.<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#52641 merged 2026-10-03 (`7dfe3338`) | Async-EPLB rebalance windows with serialized P2P batches are this opt-in. They trade per-rank contention for more sequential steps. |
| vLLM-origin NIXL host-buffer KV copy coalescing across KV cache groups | one `copy_kv_blocks` call instead of one per group | `vllm/distributed/kv_transfer/kv_connector/v1/nixl/base_worker.py`<br>Kernel/caller attribution: `vllm/v1/worker/gpu_model_runner.py` | Hybrid/multi-group models with the NIXL host buffer.<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#54483 merged 2026-10-01 (`47de9d4d`) | Fewer H2D/D2H copy launches per request sync. This is the host/offload transfer path, which the catalogs exclude by policy; it is reported here for completeness only. |

## 10. vLLM-origin status-audited PR evidence

| Pattern | Trace keywords | Primary code | Existing path | Skill should conclude |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Closed-unmerged PR `#35968` DSV3.2 multi-stream indexer overlap | `weights_proj`<br>`wk`<br>`k_norm`<br>`aux_stream` | `PR #35968`<br>`vllm/model_executor/models/deepseek_v2.py`<br>`vllm/utils/torch_utils.py` | Closed PR explored overlapping the small `weights_proj` GEMM with `wk + k_norm` on a secondary CUDA stream for decode batches instead of serializing both on the default stream | Treat this as a concrete upstream decode-time kernel-overlap family when traces show underutilized projection overlap opportunities. |
| Closed-unmerged PR `#39301` GLM5 router GEMM with PDL overlap | `TRTLLM_ENABLE_PDL`<br>`router_gemm`<br>`GLM5`<br>`FI AR RMS fusion` | `PR #39301`<br>`vllm/model_executor/layers/fused_moe/router/gate_linear.py`<br>`vllm/model_executor/layers/fused_moe/router/gate_linear.py` | The closed-unmerged experiment used PDL so the router kernel could overlap with the preceding fused allreduce-plus-RMS block | Retain this only as historical evidence; do not report it as shipped or open. |

## 11. Important toggles and caveats

| Toggle / env | Location | Effect on trace interpretation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `enable_single_batch_overlap` | `python/sglang/srt/arg_groups/fields/exec_.py` | Enables the SBO family. |
| `SGLANG_BLACKWELL_OVERLAP_SHARED_EXPERTS_OUTSIDE_SBO` | `python/sglang/srt/environ.py` | Alters how DeepSeek-style shared-expert overlap behaves on Blackwell. |
| `SGLANG_DISAGG_STAGING_BUFFER` | `python/sglang/srt/environ.py` | Enables the heterogeneous-TP staging-buffer family and its overlap windows. |
| `SGLANG_STAGING_USE_TORCH` | `python/sglang/srt/disaggregation/common/staging_buffer.py` | Forces torch fallback for staging gather / scatter, so Triton staging kernels may disappear by design. |
| `SGLANG_VIT_ENABLE_CUDA_GRAPH` | `python/sglang/srt/environ.py` | Can intentionally disable vision `aux_stream` overlap. |
| Kimi K3 overlap-slack accounting | `python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py`<br>GPU trace | A side-stream kernel only removes the portion hidden beneath the longer branch and may contend for SM, cache, or bandwidth. Prefer synchronization-point work on the critical path; reject overlap whose join moves the critical path or whose exposed savings do not exceed launch/sync overhead. |
| Kimi K3 CUDA Graph stream topology | `python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/multimodal/kimi_k3_vit_cuda_graph_runner.py` | Auxiliary streams, waits, and joins must be created and replayed unconditionally with fixed topology; every tensor produced off-stream must remain live and be recorded on its consumer stream. Exact-shape ViT graphs remain bounded and fall back eagerly. |
| Kimi K3 PDL ordering | `python/sglang/kernels/ops/attention/set_mla_kv_concat_q.py`<br>`python/sglang/kernels/jit/csrc/kimi_k3/` | The producer may signal only after all data and barrier state visible to the consumer are published. Check barrier generation and producer/consumer source order; an overlapping trace alone cannot prove race freedom. |
| Symmetric-memory logical segment parity | `python/sglang/kernels/ops/communication/all_reduce_residual.py`<br>`python/sglang/kernels/ops/communication/sp_collective.py` | Same-sized allocations can still bind different logical segments when rank control flow diverges. Use a persistent rank-symmetric arena or a collective fallback rather than relying on per-call allocation order. |
| DCP virtual cache locations | `python/sglang/kernels/ops/attention/dcp_kernels.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py` | Position ownership and physical slot numbering differ across ranks. Validate virtual-to-owner mapping before overlapping cache writes or collectives; a locally valid index can corrupt another request globally. |
| `enable_pdl` / `launch_with_pdl` | `flashinfer/norm/__init__.py`<br>`flashinfer/activation.py`<br>`flashinfer/rope.py`<br>`flashinfer/fused_moe/core.py`<br>`flashinfer/comm/allreduce.py` | Enables FlashInfer PDL across many kernels; launch grouping and same-stream overlap can change substantially when it is on. |
| `trigger_completion_at_end` | `flashinfer/comm/allreduce.py` | `False` enables downstream PDL-aware overlap after FlashInfer allreduce fusion; `True` delays completion to kernel end and removes that overlap window. |
| `use_cuda_graph` | `flashinfer/fused_moe/cute_dsl/fused_moe.py` | Enables the preallocated-buffer path and the safe aux-stream async-memset overlap in FlashInfer CuTeDSL MoE. |
| `split_device_green_ctx*` | `flashinfer/green_ctx.py` | Changes trace shape by partitioning SMs into separate green contexts instead of overlapping full-device streams on the default context. |
| `enable_torch_compile` | `python/sglang/srt/arg_groups/fields/exec_.py`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/server_args/server_args.py` | Compiler-generated reordering can hide or rename overlap windows. |
| `enable_fused_grouped_gemm_combine` | `PR #21877` | In-flight path that intentionally disables SBO because combine is folded into down-GEMM. |
| `PassConfig.enable_sp` | `vllm/config/compilation.py` | Enables vLLM's sequence-parallel staging family that creates RS / AG overlap opportunities. |
| `PassConfig.fuse_gemm_comms` | `vllm/config/compilation.py` | Enables AsyncTP GEMM + collective overlap and auto-enables `enable_sp` when valid. |
| TokenSpeed `--comm-fusion-max-num-tokens` / `--enable-allreduce-fusion` | `python/tokenspeed/runtime/utils/server_args.py`<br>`docs/configuration/server.md` | Gates TokenSpeed communication fusion; inspect it before treating all-reduce + compute separation as a new overlap gap. |

### Refreshed dispatch/launch controls

| Toggle / env | Location | Effect on trace interpretation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `FLASHINFER_ALLOW_EXPERIMENTAL_AUTO_BACKENDS=1` | `flashinfer/api_logging.py` | Lets `backend="auto"` (and autotune) choose experimental or Cake backends. Without it, auto never picks `kernel_cake_*` programs, and only an explicit `backend="cake"` (or similar) does.; evidence: #4880 merged 2026-09-04 [0.7.0] |
| `backend="cake"` / `"cake_cute"` / `"cutedsl_low_latency"` / `"b12x"` / `"cutile"` / `"cute-dsl-native"` | `flashinfer/gemm/gemm_base.py`, `flashinfer/sparse.py`, `flashinfer/comm/trtllm_ar.py`, `flashinfer/comm/trtllm_moe_alltoall.py`, `flashinfer/concat_ops.py`, `flashinfer/fused_moe/fused_routing_dsv3.py` | New explicit backend names. The same public API produces entirely different kernel names (`kernel_cake_*`, cuTile, b12x).; evidence: #4685, #4838, #5100, #4730, #4784, #4860, #4587, #5596 |
| `moe_finalize_backend` (`"trtllm"`/`"cake"`) | `flashinfer/comm/allreduce.py::allreduce_fusion` | Switches `kMoEFinalizeARResidualRMSNorm` between `moefinalize_allreduce_fusion_kernel_oneshot_lamport` and `kernel_cake_trtllm_moe_finalize_*`.; evidence: #4875 2026-09-12 |
| `use_fused_finalize` (CuTe-DSL MoE arg) ← SGLang `SGLANG_FLASHINFER_MOE_FUSED_FINALIZE` | `flashinfer/fused_moe/cute_dsl/fused_moe.py`; `python/sglang/srt/layers/moe/moe_runner/flashinfer_cutedsl.py` | False → separate finalize kernel (`moe_unpermute`) and no aux-stream memset. True → atomic fused finalize plus aux-stream memset overlap.; evidence: #5319 2026-10-01; sgl-project/sglang#40105 |
| `FLASHINFER_RAGGED_AUTO_BACKEND_ORDER` | `flashinfer/prefill.py` | Overrides the Blackwell ragged-prefill auto order (`cudnn,cutlass`). Use it to reproduce FA2 versus cuDNN/CUTLASS kernel choices in a trace.; evidence: #5133 2026-09-15 |
| `FLASHINFER_CUDNN_PREFILL_SHAPE_OVERRIDE` | `flashinfer/cudnn/prefill.py` | Controls the cuDNN ragged graph built once per length class with an execute-time shape override. Affects plan/host gaps.; evidence: #5245 2026-09-16 |
| `FLASHINFER_ALLOW_CUDNN_PLAN_BUILD_IN_CAPTURE` | `flashinfer/gemm/gemm_base.py` | Downgrades the error raised when a cuDNN GEMM plan would be built inside CUDA-graph capture. When set, expect capture-time host work.; evidence: present at SHA (no PR located; unsure) |
| `FLASHINFER_USE_CUDA_NORM=1` | `flashinfer/norm/__init__.py` | Forces CUDA JIT norm kernels instead of CuTe-DSL. This also happens automatically when the DSL cannot target the arch. Kernel names change.; evidence: pre-window; arch fallback refreshed in the 0.7 line |
| `FLASHINFER_TOPK_ALGO` (`default`/`clusters`/`cub`) | `flashinfer/topk.py` | Pins the top-k / page-table-transform algorithm, so CUB `DeviceBatchedTopK` and radix kernels swap.; evidence: #4442 2026-08-27 |
| `FLASHINFER_DISABLE_TINYGEMM2_SM100=1` | `flashinfer/gemm/routergemm.py` | Disables the SM100 Cake `tinygemm2` route, so router GEMM kernel names change.; evidence: #4274 2026-08-04 (pre-window) |
| `FLASHINFER_KDA_T1_FAST_PATH=0` | `flashinfer/kda_kernels/recurrent_kda.py` | Disables the `T=1` packed KDA decode fast path.; evidence: KDA family (#4709 / #6005) |
| `FLASHINFER_NVFP4_4OVER6*` and the `NVFP44Over6Config` arg | `flashinfer/activation.py`, `flashinfer/fused_moe/api.py`, `flashinfer/fused_moe/cute_dsl/fused_moe.py` | Selects the NVFP4 "4over6" scale recipe. Same kernels, different numerics and an extra scoring pass.; evidence: #5152 2026-09-29 (public API) |
| `FLASHINFER_MEGA_FUSED_STAGE` (default 1) | `flashinfer/moe_ep/backends/mega/kernel/sm100/fp8_fp4_bf16_deepgemm/staging.py` | 0 restores torch-side staging copies before MegaMoE, so extra copy kernels appear.; evidence: MegaMoE family |
| `FLASHINFER_SM90_CAKE_BF16_FUSED_DISPATCH` / `FLASHINFER_SM90_CAKE_BF16_FUSED_TAIL` | `flashinfer/moe_ep/kernel_src/sm90/cake_bf16_megamoe/shim/cake_runner.py` | Toggle fused dispatch and the combine tail in the SM90 Cake BF16 MegaMoE (`kernel_cake_sm90_bf16_megamoe_*`).; evidence: #5958 2026-10-02 |
| `FLASHINFER_MOE_EP_EXCLUDE_PADDING_ROWS=1` | `flashinfer/moe_ep/backends/split/kernel/fused_moe/backend.py` | Excludes padded rows from MoE-EP expert compute (#5265). Changes GEMM M in traces.; evidence: #5265 2026-09-29 |
| `FLASHINFER_DIST_AWARE_AUTOTUNE` | `flashinfer/fused_moe/da_config.py` | Distribution-aware TRT-LLM MoE autotune: the selected tactic depends on the observed routing distribution.; evidence: #4106 2026-08-13 |
| `autotune_v2` / `FLASHINFER_AUTOTUNE_CACHE_DIR` / `FLASHINFER_TACTICS_BLOCKLIST` | `flashinfer/autotune_cache.py`, `flashinfer/autotuner/autotuner.py` | Managed, persisted, deployment-matched tactic caches. A trace's GEMM/MoE tactic depends on the cache contents. SGLang syncs tactics across TP ranks (sgl-project/sglang#35343) and merges caches across spec workers (sgl-project/sglang#39678).; evidence: #3861 2026-09-04; #5705 |
| `FLASHINFER_TRACE_APPLY=1` / `FLASHINFER_TRACE_APPLY_PATH` | `flashinfer/__init__.py`, `flashinfer/trace_apply/apply.py` | Runtime kernel substitution for `fi_trace` definitions. Kernels in a profiler trace may come from substituted "solutions", not FlashInfer's own implementation. `flashinfer.trace_apply.stats()` gives hit and fallback counts.; evidence: #3240 2026-06-09 (pre-window) |
| `enable_pdl` clamp on SM107 (not an env) | `flashinfer/fused_moe/core.py` | TRT-LLM-gen MoE ignores `enable_pdl=True` on Rubin.; evidence: #4806 2026-09-02 |
| `skip_all_rows_active_check` (default now True) | `flashinfer/prefill.py` | Removes the TRT-LLM ragged active-row host check. A host gap before ragged attention means an explicit `False`.; evidence: #4931, #5039 |
| `FLASHINFER_DCP_GENERATED` / `FLASHINFER_ULYSSES_GENERATED` (compile defines) | `flashinfer/jit/cake_dcp_alltoall.py`, `flashinfer/jit/cake_ulysses.py` | Mark the Cake-generated A2A builds (`kernel_cake_dcp_alltoall_*`, `kernel_cake_ulysses_a2a_*`) versus the hand-written kernels.; evidence: #5512, #5263 |

| `TLLM_HANG_DIAGNOSTICS=1` | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/hang_diagnostics.py` | Silent unless a hang occurs, then dumps worker stacks and executor state.; evidence: `#19045` 2026-09-22 |

| `VLLM_USE_BREAKABLE_CUDAGRAPH` (auto-set per architecture) | `vllm/config/vllm.py::DEFAULT_BREAKABLE_CUDAGRAPH_ARCHITECTURES`, `vllm/v1/executor/ray_env_utils.py` | Auto-enabled for listed architectures (Dots3 NOTE added). Kernels migrated into `VllmJitKernel` classes use a `@triton.jit` / `@cute.kernel` method named `kernel`. At the frozen SHA that is 21 Triton classes (e.g. `ComputeSlotMappingKernel`, `CompressedSlotMappingKernel`, `PrepareUniformDecodeKernel`, `FusedQKVRMSNormKernel`, `FusedInvRopeFP8QuantKernel`, `CorrectAttnCPOutKernel`, `PackSeqTritonKernel`) and 22 CuTe-DSL classes. Profiler names are therefore generic `kernel`; attribute them by stack or class. Inferred from Triton naming kernels after `fn.__name__`, not observed in a captured trace. #50175 in this slice renamed `_compressed_slot_mapping_kernel`, `_prepare_uniform_decode_kernel`, `_compute_swa_indices_and_lens_kernel`, `_compute_dspark_noncausal_swa_indices_kernel` and `_convert_req_index_to_global_index_kernel`; evidence: #50175 merged 2026-09-01 (series #53564 / #53565 / #50176 / #53566 / #56323 / #50178 through 2026-09-13; infra #50174 2026-08-17) |

Map by grid shape and position, or use `record_function` / NVTX. This is unverified on a live trace; it follows from Triton naming compiled kernels after `fn.__name__`. CuTe-DSL kernels in this framework are also named `kernel` or `device_kernel`. Kernels registered via `triton_kernel_dispatcher_with_warmup` (sampling, #56323) keep their original names.; evidence: #53564, #50176, #53566, #56323 |

| MXFP8 linear kernel chosen per layer by `(N,K)` | `vllm/model_executor/kernels/linear/__init__.py::init_mxfp8_linear_kernel`, `vllm/model_executor/kernels/linear/mxfp8/flashinfer.py::_check_mm_mxfp8_shape` | Layers with K<128, N<128, K%32≠0, or SM12x N%32≠0 fall back from FlashInfer `mm_mxfp8`, so mixed MXFP8 kernel families within one model are expected.; evidence: #54223 merged 2026-09-25 |
