Its scratch is now bounded by `max_prefill_seq_len/page_size` table entries, so the dequant kernel no longer touches the whole block table.; evidence: #57918 merged 2026-09-24 |
| Gemma4 on SM90 + FP8 KV | `vllm/model_executor/models/config.py::Gemma4Config`, `vllm/v1/attention/backends/flash_attn.py::_get_sm90_fa4_fp8_kv_block_size` | Per-layer FA3 (head_dim ≤256) and FA4 (head_dim 512, FP8-KV dequant, 64-token block) instead of all-FA4. A mixed FA3/FA4 kernel list is intended.; evidence: #53175 merged 2026-09-23 |
| ROCm `kv_cache_dtype=turboquant_*` | `vllm/platforms/rocm.py::_shares_layout_with_turboquant` | Native-dtype boundary layers are restricted to backends that share a KV layout with TURBOQUANT, which can change the boundary-layer attention kernel.; evidence: #54988 merged 2026-09-23 |
| ROCm Triton 3.8 / `triton_kernels` 3.8 (gpt-oss MoE) | `vllm/model_executor/layers/fused_moe/experts/gpt_oss_triton_kernels_moe.py::UnfusedOAITritonExperts`, `vllm/model_executor/kernels/linear/mxfp8/rocm_native.py` | With tk 3.8, activation and top-k reduce are unfused from the matmuls and combined via `index_add`; LoRA is now supported on that path. gfx950 MXFP8 BK256 uses 2 stages.; evidence: #50605 merged 2026-09-25 |
| AITER v0.1.23: a16w4 MoE forced to the Triton backend on gfx950 | `vllm/model_executor/layers/fused_moe/experts/aiter_mxfp4_w4a16_moe.py` | gfx950 `moe_gemm_a16w4` runs AITER's Triton kernel, not the Gluon one. gfx1250 stays on auto.; evidence: #58867 merged 2026-09-27 |
| MRV2 + `CompilationMode.STOCK_TORCH_COMPILE` | `vllm/config/vllm.py::_disable_cudagraphs_for_v2_stock_torch_compile`, `vllm/compilation/wrapper.py::compile_model_with_stock_torch` | Now supported on MRV2, but CUDA graphs are disabled, matching V1. Expect no graph replay.; evidence: #59079 merged 2026-09-28 |
| `VLLM_SSM_CONV_STATE_LAYOUT` auto-selection on CPU | `vllm/platforms/cpu.py` | With NIXL connectors on CPU, the conv-state layout becomes DS. Otherwise it is SD on AVX512-BF16. An explicit setting wins.; evidence: #53300 merged 2026-09-24 |
| DCP-sharded target with a replicated (non-DCP) DSpark draft (`KVCacheSpec.dcp_sharded=False`) | `vllm/v1/kv_cache_interface.py`, `vllm/distributed/parallel_state.py::get_dcp_world_size_and_rank`, `vllm/v1/worker/gpu/spec_decode/speculator.py` | Under DCP the draft KV cache can be replicated, so draft attention has no DCP all-to-all and LSE combine. The draft KV is not sharded, a capacity cost.; evidence: #56723 merged 2026-09-26 |
| `VLLM_FLASHINFER_MOE_A2A_LOW_PRECISION_COMBINE` (default 0) | `vllm/envs.py`; `vllm/distributed/device_communicators/all2all.py` | FP8 combine payload in FlashInfer one-sided MoE a2a. The combine kernel changes (higher register use) and the combine leg uses half the bytes.; evidence: #57995 (2026-10-02) |
| `VLLM_DEEPEP_V2_ALLOW_HYBRID_MODE` (default **0→1**) | `vllm/envs.py`; `vllm/utils/nccl.py` | DeepEPv2 auto-selects hybrid NVLink+RDMA across NVLink domains. To compare with older traces, set it to 0.; evidence: #57991 (2026-09-29) |
| `--moe-backend flashinfer_moe_ep_cutedsl` (new) / `flashinfer_moe_ep_mega_deep_gemm`; `all2all_backend="passthrough"` (auto-bound, not on CLI) | `vllm/config/kernel.py`; `vllm/config/parallel.py`; `vllm/engine/arg_utils.py` | MoE dispatch, GEMMs and combine run in one megakernel, so the trace has no framework all2all. Any explicit `--all2all-backend` other than the default raises. Startup log: "moe_backend=…: using all2all_backend='passthrough'…".; evidence: #54049 (2026-10-02) |
| `KernelConfig.sparse_indexer_topk_backend="aiter"` | `vllm/config/kernel.py`; `vllm/model_executor/layers/indexer_topk.py` | Opt-in AITER `top_k_per_row_decode` on gfx950. The priority chain cooperative→persistent→per_row is unchanged, and aiter/deep_select/flashinfer/torch are opt-in only.; evidence: #58167 (2026-10-02) |
| `additional_config.kda_spec_decode_backend` (`auto`/`native`/`flashinfer`) | `vllm/models/kimi_k3/nvidia/kda.py` | Selects the FlashInfer packed fused KDA verify kernel for K3 MTP on SM10x. `auto` picks it when eligible.; evidence: #54255 (2026-10-01) |
| `--mamba-backend flashinfer` + `--replayssm-buffer-len` (≤16, ≥1+num_spec) + `--mamba-cache-mode none` | `vllm/config/vllm.py`; `vllm/config/cache.py` | Required for Mamba2 ReplaySSM MTP. Otherwise config validation raises. Uniform batches use dense (B,T) FlashInfer tactics.; evidence: #52928 (2026-09-30) |
| `--mamba-cache-mode` value `all` **removed** (now `align`/`none`) | `vllm/config/cache.py` | Position-indexed SSM state caching is gone. Old traces that show per-block mamba state copies with mode `all` cannot be reproduced.; evidence: #58997 (2026-10-01) |
| `VLLM_GDN_DECODE_KERNEL` (`cuda` default) now only affects **spec-decode-configured** models | `vllm/envs.py`; `vllm/model_executor/layers/mamba/gdn/qwen_gdn_linear_attn.py` | Non-spec GDN decode always uses the Triton FLA path.; evidence: #59735 (2026-10-02) |
| `VLLM_FLASHINFER_ALLREDUCE_BACKEND=auto` semantics | `vllm/distributed/device_communicators/flashinfer_all_reduce.py` | Node-local uses trtllm. Multi-node uses mnnvl on SM100+ and is disabled on Hopper.; evidence: #56891 (2026-10-03) |
| `VLLM_ROCM_USE_AITER_MOE_A4W4_DSV4=1` | `vllm/envs.py`; `vllm/model_executor/layers/fused_moe/config.py` | MXFP4 activation quant in AITER MoE for DSV4.1 only, and changes the weight shuffle layout.; evidence: #58819 (2026-09-30) |
| `--quantization-config.moe.weight int4_per_group_32` on gfx942 (Kimi-K3) | `vllm/model_executor/layers/quantization/mxfp4.py` | Lossy MXFP4→int4 requant, which brings in AITER a16wmix MoE kernels.; evidence: #51274 (2026-10-03) |
| `--kv-cache-dtype nvfp4` (MiniMax-M3 sparse MSA) | `vllm/models/minimax_m3/common/sparse_attention.py` | SM100 MSA only. The fused insert quantizes K/V, and decode is always CUTLASS (no Triton).; evidence: #59300 (2026-10-01) |
| `VLLM_RANDOMIZE_DP_DUMMY_INPUTS` (with DP>1) | `vllm/envs.py`; `vllm/v1/worker/gpu_worker.py::randomize_dummy_inputs` | Idle-DP dummy batches and the profile run use random token ids, so MoE routing in dummy steps is spread instead of all-zero, which sends every token to the same experts.; evidence: #58411 (2026-10-01) |
| `PoolerConfig.enable_flash_late_interaction` / `--no-enable-flash-late-interaction` | `vllm/config/pooler.py`; `vllm/entrypoints/launchers/cli_args.py` | Toggles the fused flash-maxsim scorer.; evidence: #40337 (2026-10-01) |
| `EPLBConfig.enable_migration_batching` (default off, async EPLB) | `vllm/config/parallel.py` | Expert migrations run as conflict-free P2P batches, annotated with the trace range `eplb: schedule migration batches`.; evidence: #52641 (2026-10-03) |
| `ModelConfig.sleep_mode_offload_cudagraph` / `--sleep-mode-offload-cudagraph`; `enable_nccl_comm_suspend` / `--enable-nccl-comm-suspend` | `vllm/config/model.py`; `vllm/compilation/cudagraph_pool.py` | Graphs are captured into a cuMem pool that is backed up on sleep. This adds one extra copy per captured custom all-reduce (~3% decode latency at bs1) and defaults `NCCL_GRAPH_REGISTER=0`.; evidence: #59160 (2026-10-04) |
| `pcp_shard_decode_requests` (derived: PCP>1 and DCP==1) | `vllm/config/parallel.py` | Each PCP rank holds a partial decode batch, plus extra decode-KV gathers.; evidence: #52162 (2026-10-01) |
| SM120 Triton attention `num_par_softmax_segments` (auto 64/16) | `vllm/v1/attention/backends/triton_attn.py` | Different 3D grid and `reduce_segments` work on SM120 depending on occupancy. There is no user toggle.; evidence: #58482 (2026-10-01) |
| W4A16 NVFP4 `--linear-backend auto` prefers FlashInfer CuTe-DSL on SM100/103 (Marlin elsewhere) | `vllm/model_executor/layers/quantization/modelopt.py` | The docs and comment now match the code. On Blackwell, a Marlin W4A16 NVFP4 kernel means auto was overridden or CuTe-DSL is unavailable.; evidence: #59135 (2026-10-02, docs + comment) |
| QuantFP8 native per-token path uses `amax` (value-only) instead of `max(dim)` | `vllm/model_executor/layers/quantization/input_quant_fp8.py` | The torch-native/compiled per-token FP8 quant emits an amax reduction without an indices output. Pattern-matcher consistency is preserved because `MatcherQuantFP8` traces the same `forward_native`.; evidence: #59800 (2026-10-02) |
| Triton sampler `do_not_specialize_on_alignment` (`BATCH_SIZE`, `ROUND`) | `vllm/v1/sample/ops/topk_topp_triton.py` (`_topk_topp_kernel`, `_topp_sb_step_kernel`) | Fewer JIT specializations during warmup. This reduces startup and first-batch compile stalls, with no kernel-time change.; evidence: #58605 (2026-09-29) |
| ROCm AITER MLA FP8 prefill plan staged via pinned host buffers (2 slots + events) | `vllm/v1/attention/backends/mla/rocm_aiter_mla.py` | Under async scheduling there is an extra H2D copy on the current stream and an occasional event wait (`gpu_sync_allowed`). This is a correctness fix, so do not flag it as a new host gap.; evidence: #58887 (2026-10-01) |

## 12. Suggested refresh commands

These are source-inspection commands, not serving flags. Inspect immutable refs
and compare consumer pins before treating a main-built dependency as shipped.

```bash
SGLANG_REPO=${SGLANG_REPO:-../sglang}
VLLM_REPO=${VLLM_REPO:-../vllm}
TRTLLM_REPO=${TRTLLM_REPO:-../TensorRT-LLM}
TOKENSPEED_REPO=${TOKENSPEED_REPO:-../tokenspeed}
FLASHINFER_REPO=${FLASHINFER_REPO:-../flashinfer}
SGLANG_REF=b1bbd74f287f13ed1276b0403a01ebb55c597e93
VLLM_REF=0c16eee3f1ff777298cc894c3eeb85f3880c6d6a
TRTLLM_REF=bb367fc8c1adf6e2c28c88cb1a8b46e1742a9d60
TOKENSPEED_REF=6fa10840d5c3c23065f60428ad264fba60fa04ae
FLASHINFER_REF=724e238e7deca2d6ffd5668d63ae95c428414f59

git -C "$SGLANG_REPO" grep -n -E 'fused|overlap|enable_pdl|cuda_graph|alt_stream|symm_mem|DeepEP|MegaMoE' "$SGLANG_REF" -- python/sglang/kernels python/sglang/srt python/sglang/multimodal_gen
git -C "$VLLM_REPO" grep -n -E 'FusionPass|Pattern|aux_stream|maybe_execute_in_parallel|cudagraph|enable_pdl|deepep_v2' "$VLLM_REF" -- vllm/compilation vllm/models vllm/model_executor vllm/v1 vllm/distributed csrc/libtorch_stable
git -C "$TRTLLM_REPO" grep -n -E 'fusion_op|AuxStreamType|multi_stream|enable_pdl|prefill_cuda_graph|MegaMoE' "$TRTLLM_REF" -- tensorrt_llm/_torch tensorrt_llm/llmapi cpp/tensorrt_llm
git -C "$TOKENSPEED_REPO" grep -n -E 'StreamFork|enable_pdl|prefill_graph|comm_fusion|replay|mega_moe|rl-bitwise' "$TOKENSPEED_REF" -- python/tokenspeed tokenspeed-kernel tokenspeed-kernel-amd tokenspeed-mla
git -C "$FLASHINFER_REPO" grep -n -E 'AllReduceFusionPattern|enable_pdl|use_fused_finalize|kernel_cake_|MoEEp|split_device_green_ctx' "$FLASHINFER_REF" -- flashinfer include csrc
# Compare with the deployment pin, not just FlashInfer main:
git -C "$FLASHINFER_REPO" diff --stat v0.7.0.post1 "$FLASHINFER_REF" -- flashinfer include
# When refreshing further, inspect relevant diffs rather than assuming a title proves fusion:
# git -C "$SGLANG_REPO" log --first-parent --since=2026-10-05 --format='%H %cs %s' HEAD
# git -C "$SGLANG_REPO" show <candidate_sha> -- <source_path>
```

## 13. GPU idle-gap / launch-overhead families

These paths reduce repeated launches, metadata work, CPU/GPU synchronization or exposed communication and copies. Compare critical-path gaps with the same shapes and numerical policy. CPU config/host ranges are caller clues rather than GPU kernel names.

| Pattern | Trace keywords | Primary code | Existing path | Skill should conclude |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Host-sync and plan-gap removal that shows as GPU idle (attention planning) | `skip_all_rows_active_check`<br>`_blackwell_ragged_auto_upgrade`<br>`trtllm_ragged_attention_deepseek`<br>`fast_decode_plan` | `flashinfer/prefill.py`<br>`flashinfer/decode.py`<br>`flashinfer/cudnn/prefill.py` | Removed host syncs: the `.item()` readback skipped under graph capture (#4703), the TRT-LLM ragged active-row check skipped by default (#4931, #5039), one `Tensor.max()` in `plan()` (#5043), CPU seq-len mirrors consumed (#4816), the cuDNN ragged graph built once per length class (#5245), cuDNN plan reuse (#5350), cuDNN paged-prefill lengths staged without a host sync (#5976), CUTLASS ragged plan-once under CUDA graph (#5975).<br>Evidence: #4703 2026-08-29; #4931 2026-09-04; #5039 (cherry-picked) [0.7.0]; #4816, #5043, #5245, #5350 [0.7.1rc]; #5975, #5976 2026-10-03 | GPU idle before attention in a FlashInfer trace on 0.7.1rc1/rc2 can be the #5350/#5133 regressions fixed in rc3 / #5975–#5976. On 0.7.0.post1, #5039 is present but #5043, #5245 and #5350 are not. |
| Device-side scheduling and persistent kernels replacing host plans or multi-launch chains | `kernel_cake_fmha_decode_balanced_*`<br>`kernel_cake_vsa_sm90_*`<br>`BatchAttention`<br>`sm110_gqa_decode` | `flashinfer/experimental/balanced_gqa_decode/`<br>`flashinfer/cake_vsa_sm90.py`<br>`flashinfer/attention/_core.py`<br>`flashinfer/experimental/sm110_gqa_decode/` | Device-side load-balanced decode (#5474), a queue-scheduled persistent VSA stage (#5687), persistent `BatchAttention` specialized for equal KV strides (#5329), the SM110 GQA decode with no per-call host sync (#5929), stream-K tails in the Cake GEMMs (#5944), and lower non-CP GDN CuTe-DSL launch overhead (#4699).<br>Evidence: #4699 2026-08-24; #4736 (cherry-picked) [0.7.0]; #5329, #5474, #5687 [0.7.1rc]; #5929, #5944 [main] | Many short attention or GEMM launches with host gaps have persistent upstream counterparts. Cite the specific backend. |
| CUDA-graph capture enablers (keep fused paths inside graphs) | `MoEEpSplitGraphState`<br>`block_valid_mask`<br>`FLASHINFER_ALLOW_CUDNN_PLAN_BUILD_IN_CAPTURE` | `flashinfer/moe_ep/modes/split_layer.py`<br>`flashinfer/gemm/gemm_base.py`<br>`flashinfer/topk.py` | Graph-capturable EP split layer (#4795, #5287), padded graph prefill with a materialized `block_valid_mask` (#5176), graph-safe page-table top-k via a CUB fallback on SM120 (#5226), graph-safe KDA prefix checkpoints (#5021), Cake MoE-AR scratch retained across captured graphs (#5894), and a guard against building a cuDNN GEMM plan during capture.<br>Evidence: #4795, #5021 [0.7.0]; #5176, #5226, #5287 [0.7.1rc]; #5894 [main] | When a FlashInfer path falls back to eager or is missing from a graph, check these guards first. |
| Breakable CUDA graph (BCG) for diffusion DiTs | `[Diffusion BCG] captured`<br>`[Diffusion BCG] serving signature MISSED`<br>`cudaGraphLaunch` segments separated by eager attention / collectives | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/breakable_cuda_graph/runner.py`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/breakable_cuda_graph/prompt_padding.py`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/breakable_cuda_graph/model_padders/`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/server_args/server_args.py::_adjust_breakable_cuda_graph_support`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner_backend_utils/breakable_cuda_graph/breakable_cuda_graph.py`<br>Kernel/caller attribution: `python/sglang/kernels/ops/kvcache/trtllm_mha_graph_metadata.py` | `--enable-breakable-cuda-graph` captures fixed-signature DiT segments split at attention; supported: Anima, FLUX.1-dev, Ideogram-4, JoyAI-Echo, LTX-2, LongCat-Image(-Edit-Turbo), MiniMax-H3, Qwen-Image/2512/2.1, SANA1.5, SANA-Video, Z-Image(-Turbo), GLM-Image; mutually exclusive with `--enable-torch-compile` and Cache-DiT (BCG wins); default resolution auto-captured; other resolutions/frame counts/prompt buckets run eager<br>Evidence: #27436 merged 2026-07-08<br>#33885 2026-08-07 (LTX-2 e2e 10.75 -> 6.90 s)<br>#33989 2026-08-07 (SANA e2e -26%)<br>#34174 2026-08-10 (auto default resolution)<br>#34210 2026-08-10 (Z-Image bit-exact)<br>#34929, #35724, #35729, #35961, #36008, #36485, #37890, #38110, #38591, #40384 (2026-08-16 .. 2026-09-24)<br>#33421 2026-08-07 (BCG with TP) | A launch-bound eager DiT trace on a supported model should first try BCG; a BCG row is valid only with a `captured` log and no `MISSED` / capture-failure / eager-fallback marker; quality=high replay after a lossless capture is rejected |
| Capture-safe collectives for graph capture | `bump_signal_kernel`, `spin_wait_kernel` inside captured graphs<br>pynccl all-to-all inside graph | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/distributed/device_communicators/ipc_a2a.py`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/distributed/device_communicators/pynccl.py`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/distributed/group_coordinator.py`<br>Kernel/caller attribution: `python/sglang/kernels/jit/csrc/distributed/ipc_a2a.cuh` | Lets BCG/TP and Ulysses keep collectives capturable instead of forcing eager segments<br>Evidence: #33775 merged 2026-08-06 (capture-safe pynccl A2A)<br>#36658 merged 2026-08-28 (tail attention metadata capturable) | Unexpected eager breaks at every collective under BCG + SP/TP suggest the capture-safe path is not active |
| VLA / action-model full CUDA graphs (prefix encoding, per-step action denoise, tensor-function graphs) | `VLAPrefixGraphRunner`, `VLADenoiseGraphRunner`, `VLATensorGraphRunner` replays | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/vla/cuda_graph.py`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/pipelines/pi05.py`, `flux3_action.py` | Bounded LRU of full CUDA graphs keyed by shape bucket; does not use diffusion BCG<br>Evidence: #34256 merged 2026-08-11<br>#34599 merged 2026-08-29<br>#42171 merged 2026-10-04 | Launch-bound Pi0.5 / FLUX.3 action traces should replay these graphs; eager per-step launches can mean a signature miss |
| MiniMax-H3 AdaLN plan cache (precomputed per-block AdaLN, GPU LRU + pinned-host tier H2D on side stream) and one batched AdaLN all-gather | `MiniMax H3 AdaLN host tier: N pages`<br>H2D memcpy on the cache stream<br>single TP `all_gather` before the block loop | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/models/dits/minimax_h3_adaln_cache.py`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/models/dits/minimax_h3.py::_can_batch_block_adaln` | Removes per-block AdaLN GEMMs from the step; `SGLANG_DIFFUSION_MINIMAX_H3_ADALN_GPU_PLANS`, `--minimax-h3-adaln-cache-path`, `--minimax-h3-adaln-host-cache-gb`<br>Evidence: #37266 merged 2026-09-03<br>#34650 merged 2026-08-14<br>#34993 merged 2026-08-19 | Per-block AdaLN GEMMs or one all-gather per block in H3 traces can mean the cache/batching guard missed |
| Layerwise weight offload with copy-stream prefetch (H2D overlapped with DiT compute) | `MemcpyHtoD` on `copy_stream` overlapping block kernels; `wait_stream` joins | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/managers/memory_managers/layerwise_offload.py`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/managers/memory_managers/component_residency_strategies.py` | `--dit-layerwise-offload`, `--layerwise-prefetch-size`, resident-layer policies; `performance_mode=memory`; pinned host stores; auto-residency keeps DiT resident on large GPUs<br>Evidence: #15511 merged 2025-12-20 (overlap with compute)<br>#39021 merged 2026-09-11 (pinned in place)<br>#38441 merged 2026-09-08 (shared host/device pool)<br>#38001 merged 2026-09-05 (auto-keep DiT resident) | Exposed H2D gaps between DiT blocks = insufficient prefetch or non-pinned stores; on large GPUs auto-residency should remove them entirely |
| Per-layer attention backend autotune | backend kernel mix differs per layer (FA / cuDNN SDPA / Sage) | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/layers/attention/autotune.py`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/layers/attention/layer.py::_maybe_install_backend_autotune` | `--enable-attention-backend-autotune` (off by default; measured on SM90/SM12x); explicit backends preserved<br>Evidence: #38689 merged 2026-09-12<br>#39882 merged 2026-09-17 | Heterogeneous attention kernels across layers are intended under autotune |
| Host-gap removals in the denoise loop | fewer `cudaStreamSynchronize` / H2D in step; hoisted RoPE caches | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/models/schedulers/` (UniPC)<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/models/dits/qwen_image.py` (host-side varlen metadata)<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/models/dits/ernie_image.py`, `flux.py`, `wanvideo.py` (RoPE cos/sin hoist)<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/models/dits/qwen_image.py` (modulation cached across serial CFG branches)<br>Kernel/caller attribution: `python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/memory_pool.py` | Removes per-step GPU syncs, metadata kernels and repeated modulation GEMMs<br>Evidence: #27440 merged 2026-06-06 (UniPC syncs)<br>#33954 merged 2026-08-07 (Qwen varlen metadata host-side)<br>#27443 merged 2026-06-07 (Ideogram metadata)<br>#37090 merged 2026-08-31 (Qwen modulation across CFG)<br>#34004/#34306/#34584 (RoPE hoists) | GPU idle gaps at step boundaries should be checked against these before proposing scheduling changes |
| CUDA-graph replay input-copy batching | `_small_copy_kernel` | `python/sglang/kernels/ops/memory/small_copy.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/cuda_graph_buffer_registry.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner_utils/buffers.py` | Eligible small graph inputs copied in one Triton launch per phase (raw-bit preserving, large buffers keep DMA)<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#41166 merged 2026-09-27 | Many tiny H2D/D2D copies before each graph replay are pre-#41166 or unsupported layouts. |

Now covers eager prefill and DFLASH-family targets<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#35947 merged 2026-08-27<br>Supplement: `sgl-project/sglang#38554` merged 2026-09-14 (`9128d57966`, EAGLE/MTP)<br>`#39502` merged 2026-09-18 (`da2f434951`, plugin capability)<br>Origin #35947 (DFLASH, before this slice) | A host-side wait on the whole prefill forward before preparing the next batch, on DSV4 + DFLASH, can mean the knob is off. |
| Unified-memory allocator compaction: batched p2v gather instead of per-page `.item()` syncs | `aten::item` / `cudaStreamSynchronize` per moved page (old)<br>`_assert_async` | `python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/allocator/unified_sub_pool.py` (was ; moved by #38072 on 2026-09-04) | Survivor virtual ids are gathered from `physical_to_virtual` on device in one batch and validated with `torch._assert_async`, which removes one D2H sync per moved page during lazy compaction<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#34066 merged 2026-08-24<br>Only the verified current caller paths are retained; earlier sweep paths moved or were removed. | Repeated tiny D2H syncs during a unified-memory flush can mean a pre-#34066 build. |
| FlashInfer autotune: tactic sync across TP ranks, and EXTEND autotune for DP/PD prefill | per-rank divergent FlashInfer MoE/GEMM tactics, which show as collective skew<br>log "per-rank caches disagree" | `python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner/flashinfer_autotune.py` (`_autotune_tactic_sync_group`, `_drop_diverged_autotune_cache`, `maybe_flashinfer_autotune_extend`) | Timings are all-reduced over the TP CPU group so every rank picks the same tactic. Diverged caches are discarded. EXTEND autotune (`SGLANG_FLASHINFER_AUTOTUNE_EXTEND`, default False) sizes from `max_prefill_buffer_tokens` and now also runs for PD-prefill speculative targets<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#35343 merged 2026-08-26; #36219 merged 2026-08-25 | Different FlashInfer kernels for the same shape on different TP ranks, causing straggler skew before collectives, can mean a pre-#35343 build or an autotune cache shared across mismatched environments. |
| ROCm HIP DSA draft-extend CUDA graph; spec-adaptive graph sizing | draft-extend graph replay in place of eager on MI3xx DSA MTP<br>shared `cuda_graph_capture` stream | `python/sglang/srt/speculative/eagle_worker_v2.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner_utils/pool.py::get_or_create_global_graph_capture_stream`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/model_runner.py` | `DeepseekSparseAttnBackend` now qualifies for the HIP draft-extend graph. Adaptive spec sizes graph rows over every candidate step count, and serial capture passes share one stream to reuse allocator scratch<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#35672 merged 2026-08-25; #35275 merged 2026-08-27 | An eager draft-extend on ROCm DSA (GLM-5.x/DSV3.2) can mean a pre-#35672 build. |
| Spec-decode allocation host overhead (host-only) | per-request `int(tensor[i])` on a CPU tensor replaced by `.tolist()` | `python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/allocation.py` | Host-only. It is not a GPU sync, because the tensor is already on CPU<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#35377 merged 2026-08-26 | Minor scheduler CPU cost; do not count it as a GPU-idle fix. |
| DP1 skips the scheduler MLP-sync metadata all-gather | small NCCL `all_gather` / `all_reduce` + `cudaStreamSynchronize` before each forward (gone for DP1) | `python/sglang/srt/managers/scheduler_components/dp_attn.py::should_skip_scheduler_all_gather`<br>`python/sglang/srt/managers/scheduler_components/dp_attn.py` | Default for `dp_size == 1`; `SGLANG_SCHEDULER_SKIP_ALL_GATHER` still forces it for DP>1. PR body: 14.34 -> 12.85 ms (-10.4%) and +11.45% in the A/B.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#36568 merged 2026-08-27 (`6480cce9`) | A per-step tiny collective + host sync in DP1 TP traces can mean an older build; for DP>1 it is required unless the env guarantees invariance. Also check the frozen-head backend, shape, graph/capture and fallback guards before attributing the split to an old version. |
| SWA page expansion without `torch.unique` | `unique` + implicit sync in SWA free path disappears | `python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/allocator/swa.py` | Keeps duplicate pages (paged free dedups later); CPU reference check only in debug mode.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#37463 merged 2026-09-01 (`83a9b5dd`) | Scheduler-stream syncs in SWA free = older build. |
| HiCache prefetch termination all-reduce only for `timeout` policy | CPU (gloo) all-reduce per check in scheduler loop | `python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/unified_radix_cache.py` (`_can_terminate_prefetch`) | `best_effort` / `wait_complete` no longer pay the CPU collective.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#36425 merged 2026-08-29 (`8e04f66a`) | Host-only; scheduler-loop collective time with HiCache prefetch belongs to the timeout policy. |
| Rust radix TreeCore backend (scheduler CPU) | shorter CPU gaps in match/insert/evict | `python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/unified_cache/tree_core_registry.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/rust_tree_core/adapter.py` | `SGLANG_UNIFIED_RADIX_TREE_CORE_BACKEND` (default `python` at #32710, `rust` at frozen SHA).<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#32710 merged 2026-09-01 (`9cf157c2`) | Host-gap analysis on UnifiedRadixCache must note which TreeCore backend ran. |
| Mixed chunked prefill with speculative decoding | mixed EXTEND steps where running requests do a 1-token decode | `python/sglang/srt/speculative/spec_info.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/managers/overlap_utils.py` | `--enable-mixed-chunk` now allowed with EAGLE/EAGLE3/DFlash/DSpark (not ngram).<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#36933 merged 2026-08-31 (`07d84ebd`) | Spec traces may contain mixed prefill+decode steps without draft/verify; do not misread them as spec being off. |
| VLA Pi0.5 bounded LRU CUDA-graph cache + prompt bucketing | `cudaGraphLaunch` replays; capture misses counted in `VLAGraphCacheInfo` | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/vla/cuda_graph.py::_BoundedCaptureCache`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/vla/prompt_bucketing.py` | Bounded graph serving (evict-on-miss). PR body: request 197.3 -> 179.4 ms on H200.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#34599 merged 2026-08-29 (`0e1146d0`) | Eager Pi0.5 traces with ~10k launches per request = graph cache miss or disabled. |
| Diffusion SP tail-attention metadata made graph-capturable | no H2D copy from a Python list inside capture | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/distributed/sp_shard_utils.py::tail_attn_meta` | Builds `cu_seqlens` on device with `arange`.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#36658 merged 2026-08-28 (`7088f219`) | Capture failures/eager fallback with Ulysses padding before 2026-08-28 came from this. |
| B1. Unified-memory (virtual→physical page) metadata kernels: bind/unbind, read-table/read-stream build, write-loc translate in one launch | `alloc_bind_inplace_kernel`<br>`free_unbind_inplace_kernel`<br>`bind_inplace_kernel`<br>`build_kv_read_indices_kernel`<br>`translate_token_ids_kernel` (named `write_loc_to_kernel_id_kernel` in #37926, renamed by #40331)<br>`_fused_replay_state_indices_kernel` (`HAS_V2P`)<br>`create_mla_kv_page_table_for_dcp` (`HAS_V2P`) | `python/sglang/kernels/ops/memory/virtual_slot.py`<br>`python/sglang/kernels/ops/kvcache/kv_read_table.py`<br>`python/sglang/kernels/ops/mamba/mamba_state_indices_triton.py`<br>`python/sglang/kernels/ops/attention/dcp_kernels.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/allocator/unified_sub_pool.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/kv_index_translator.py` | `--enable-unified-memory`. The read-table grid is sized from `bs`; the column loop is bounded by device `seq_lens`, so a captured graph does not replay `max_context_len/BLOCK` blocks. The tombstone scatters are one kernel. The read stream is built CSR-direct, without the page-table rectangle. The write-loc translate (~6-12 eager ops under DCP) is one kernel. The unified mamba pool takes the fused replay state-indices path.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#37511 merged 2026-09-02 (`18d5ffb42a`); sgl-project/sglang#37512 merged 2026-09-02 (`d9848b9ecd`); sgl-project/sglang#37693 merged 2026-09-03 (`a11dba1a01`); sgl-project/sglang#37926 merged 2026-09-07 (`b5766336d4`) | On Blackwell decode with unified memory, many tiny int64 gather/div/mod kernels outside the graph (an extra ~456 `cudaLaunchKernel` per profiled window in the PR) can mean a stale build. #37926 measured unified 1.96% behind static (worst -4.06%) on B300 Kimi-Linear TP2/DCP2 `cutedsl_mla`, entirely CPU-launch bound; fixed. |
| B2. ROCm uses the fused DSA decode / target-verify / draft-extend metadata kernels; MLA absorb path skips redundant work | `fused_dsa_decode_metadata`<br>`fused_dsa_target_verify_metadata`<br>`fused_dsa_draft_extend_metadata`<br>absent on HIP: `concat_mla_absorb_q_general` when Q is already concatenated, `w_kc.to(bf16)*1.0` dequant | `python/sglang/kernels/ops/attention/dsa_metadata.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/dsa_backend.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/dsa/dsa_backend_mtp_precompute.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/models/deepseek_common/attention_forward_methods/forward_mla_rocm.py` | The gates changed from `is_cuda() and not _is_hip` to `(is_cuda() or _is_hip)` when `dsa_index_kpool <= 1`. On HIP the `w_vc` BMM writes into a preallocated buffer (`out=`) except under TC piecewise graphs.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#37124 merged 2026-09-06 (`15aa2fb843`) | On ROCm DSA (GLM-5.x / DSV3.2) traces, a burst of small metadata kernels per step now can mean an old build or `dsa_index_kpool > 1`. |
| B3. Full prefill CUDA graph (FullCG) with chunked-prefix capture variants: DP-coordinated variant choice and shared output storage | `prefill_cuda_graph_runner`<br>`full_cuda_graph_backend`<br>one extra DtoD `copy_` into the shared output buffer at graph end | `python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner/prefill_cuda_graph_runner.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner_backend/full_cuda_graph_backend.py` | Every DP rank selects the same prefix-capture variant from the global max prefix (avoids collective mismatch and eager fallback). With `reuse_output_buffer`, all captured prefill graphs write into one output buffer sized at the first (largest) capture.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#37888 merged 2026-09-04 (`fbf8f1dbf6`); sgl-project/sglang#38038 merged 2026-09-04 (`613d87becd`) | Eager prefill on some DP ranks next to graph replay on others can mean a pre-#37888 build. A trailing output copy kernel after the FullCG replay is the price of the memory reuse, not a bug. |

Diffusion breakable CUDA graph (BCG) coverage and warmup | BCG replay windows in the denoise loop<br>`_ltx2_call_current_model` | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/breakable_cuda_graph/runner.py`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/pipelines_core/stages/model_specific_stages/joy_echo/denoising.py`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/server_args/server_args.py` | `--enable-breakable-cuda-graph` now supports `jdopensource/JoyAI-Echo`. BCG warmup reports the frame-count resolution for video models. MiniMax-H3 warms up at the served clip shape.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#38110 merged 2026-09-05 (`eda10c3678`); sgl-project/sglang#37890 merged 2026-09-04 (`01e66a62db`); sgl-project/sglang#37945 merged 2026-09-04 (`3b678549c3`) | A launch-bound eager denoise on JoyEcho can mean BCG is off or an old build. A first-request capture at an unwarmed frame count shows up as a long first step; check the warmup frame-count log. |
| B6. SWA free without a data-dependent filter (no D2H sync) | absent: `nonzero`/boolean-mask `cudaMemcpyDtoH` sync in `free_swa` at page_size 1 | `python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/allocator/swa.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/allocator/swa.py` | #36723 removed the `swa_indices[swa_indices > 0]` filter at page_size == 1. Later (outside the slice) the code became fixed-shape at every page size via `free_swa_segment`.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#36723 merged 2026-09-02 (`19c7679e9e`) | A host sync inside the SWA free on hybrid-SWA models (Gemma4, GPT-OSS, DSV4) is a stale-build symptom, not an inherent cost. |

ROCm skips the unused DSV4 TOPK v2 plan kernel | absent on HIP: `topk_plan` launch in `TopKKernel` | `python/sglang/kernels/jit/csrc/deepseek_v4/topk_v2.cuh` | ROCm compiles out the cluster path, the plan's only consumer, so the plan launch now returns early under `USE_ROCM`.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#37580 merged 2026-09-04 (`7f89cc5286`) | One fewer launch per DSV4 top-k on AMD. | B14. Scheduler / allocator host-overhead reductions (host-only; may surface as GPU idle without the overlap scheduler) | CPU time in radix match / free-list `torch.cat`+`sort`; many tiny index kernels from per-request loops on NPU | `python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/radix_cache.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/allocator/paged.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/hardware_backend/npu/allocator_npu.py` | 1. The radix tree is walked by offset, without re-slicing token storage (#37324). 2. Freed pages are staged unsorted and merge-sorted only when an alloc runs short (#37146, PD `need_sort`). 3. `alloc_extend_naive` is vectorized, which removes per-request launches on NPU (#37938).<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#37324 merged 2026-09-02 (`cf3173aeb9`); sgl-project/sglang#37146 merged 2026-09-02 (`c05f8ae830`); sgl-project/sglang#37938 merged 2026-09-04 (`a44bb397a9`) | Mention only when the trace shows scheduler-side gaps. The catalog intentionally excludes pure host work. |
| SGLang CUDA-graph modes: decode `full`, prefill `breakable` (BCG) / `tc_piecewise`, per-phase config | prefill replays as graph segments with eager breaks (`is_in_breakable_cuda_graph`)<br>`[dense-decode] DSA dual-graph enabled` log (ROCm)<br>`Prefill CUDA graph captured a DP gather/scatter` log | `python/sglang/srt/model_executor/cuda_graph_config.py::Backend`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner/prefill_cuda_graph_runner.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner/decode_cuda_graph_runner.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/graph_variants.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/arg_groups/cuda_graph_hook.py`<br>Kernel/caller attribution: `python/sglang/srt/utils/common.py` | Per-phase backends: `--cuda-graph-backend-{decode,prefill}` ∈ {full, breakable, tc_piecewise, disabled}, `--cuda-graph-config` JSON. Slice changes: GLM-5.3-Flash BCG opt-in, with the KPool indexer run as an eager break and default chunk/max_bs 4096 (`#38522`); MoE-LoRA in full/breakable prefill graphs (`#38578`); unified-memory pool prefill capture (FULL only on fa3/fa4, `#37418`); shared MAX_LEN prefill bucket when a DP gather is captured (`#37933`); ROCm DSA dense/sparse decode graph variants (`#38993`, admission fix `#38992`).<br>Evidence: `#38522` merged 2026-09-10; `#38578` merged 2026-09-11 (TTFT 303.7 → 93.4 ms at bs1/512); `#37418` (`6410800af9`, 2026-09-13); `#37933` (`7b791c9534`, 2026-09-09); `#38993` (`17fa5ad327`); `#38992` (`94ce940ff8`) | Many prefill kernel launches with host gaps on supported models can mean prefill graphs are disabled or unsupported. BCG traces legitimately interleave eager segments, such as the KPool indexer. This launch-overhead family is entirely absent from the catalogs. |
| Diffusion breakable CUDA graph (BCG) allowlist and lossless-only replay | `[Diffusion BCG] captured` / `[Diffusion BCG] disabled for ...` logs; DiT segment replays | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/breakable_cuda_graph/runner.py`<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/server_args/server_args.py::_adjust_breakable_cuda_graph_support` | `--enable-breakable-cuda-graph` with `--warmup-resolutions` (and `--bcg-text-buckets`) captures DiT segments for an allowlist (Anima, FLUX.1-dev [added in this slice], Ideogram-4, JoyAI-Echo, LTX-2, LongCat-Image, MiniMax-H3, Qwen-Image family, SANA, Z-Image, GLM-Image). Requests with `quality=high/extra-high` that would mount request-scoped DiT fusions are rejected under BCG. Uncaptured signatures fall back to eager.<br>Evidence: `#38591` merged (commit `a27be5ff62`, 2026-09-09) | When diffusion launch gaps persist with BCG on, check the allowlist warning and whether the resolution was warmed. BCG and quality fusions are mutually exclusive, so a fusion missing from a BCG trace is intended. |
| CUDA-graph executable dedup and graph-pool borrowing | fewer `cudaGraphExec` instances; `Graph pool borrow capacity: X MiB available, largest contiguous region Y MiB` log | `python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner_backend/cuda_graph_dedup_mixin.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner_utils/pool.py` | Dedup registration now reuses the live executable via `cudaGraphExecUpdate` (the separate compat exec is gone; preferred-cluster mismatches are still keyed by handle, ref `#37657`). Graph-pool borrowing is runtime-scoped, exposes a capacity check, and keeps borrows on their allocation stream.<br>Evidence: `#39176` (`2784a86062`), `#39177` (`7ae4af8187`), `#39178` (`18cc55dc0b`), `#39180` (`206034e520`), all 2026-09-12 | This is memory/startup rather than GPU-time behaviour, but it changes the post-capture free memory that sizes the KV pool. Read the new borrow-capacity log line during capacity triage. |
| ROCm DSV4 DSPARK target-verify metadata built inside the graph (host-bubble removal) | `_expand_prefill_causally_kernel` (device cumsum) replacing per-request CPU loop / implicit D2H | `python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/deepseek_v4_backend_hip_radix.py`<br>`python/sglang/kernels/ops/attention/dsv4_attn_metadata_kernels.py::ExpandPrefillCausally` | For DSPARK uniform verify blocks, raw-verify metadata expansion is deferred into the prefill/verify CUDA graph (eager for EAGLE and ragged layouts; bounded by the FP4-indexer `MAX_FUSED_ROWS`). `exact_num_tokens` skips a D2H.<br>Evidence: `#39116` merged (commit `21289cfd50`, 2026-09-12) | CPU gaps before DSV4 verify on MI35x DSPARK came from builds before `#39116`, or an EAGLE/ragged layout. |
| Hybrid-SWA page free without device-to-host sync (`page_size > 1`) | absence of `nonzero` / boolean-mask D2H sync in SWA free; `free_page_ids` | `python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/allocator/swa.py::_free_swa_pages`<br>`python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/common.py::free_swa_out_of_window_slots` | One representative slot per page is freed (`free_index[::page_size]`), giving fixed-shape frees at every page size, deferred into the free group. This replaces `swa_indices[swa_indices > 0]` filtering, which forced a sync.<br>Evidence: `#38159` merged 2026-09-08 (`28ebede865`) | Per-step `cudaStreamSynchronize` / `aten::nonzero` stalls in SWA models (Gemma/GPT-OSS/MiMo-style) with `page_size > 1` came from builds before `#38159`. |

This allows `return_sampling_mask` with the overlap scheduler. New `--sampling-mask-max-tokens` (default 4096).<br>Evidence: `#36631` merged (commit `fd7743e0e1`, 2026-09-10) | Host syncs in the sampler when sampling masks are requested came from builds before `#36631`. Requests whose mask exceeds the limit are aborted. |
| Diffusion layerwise offload: H2D prefetch on a copy stream and mapped-weight courier (shared host/device pool) | `Memcpy HtoD` on `copy_stream` / courier stream overlapping DiT compute; `MADV_WILLNEED` readahead | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/managers/memory_managers/layerwise_offload.py` (`LayerwiseOffloadManager`, `MappedLayerCourier`) | Layers are prefetched `prefetch_size` ahead on a dedicated stream with per-layer events. On unified-memory boxes (GB10), weights stream from their safetensors mapping into pinned slots instead of a duplicate pinned copy (`#38441`). Host stores are pinned in place (`#39021`), and mapped layers are read directly when the host cannot cache them (`#39022`).<br>Evidence: `#38441` merged 2026-09-08 (`775f17b07c`; 1.58–2.0× per request on DGX Spark MiniMax-H3); `#39021` (`ab9750fb35`), `#39022` (`bf3305b65e`) 2026-09-11/12 | When DiT kernels wait on H2D copies, the prefetch window is too small or the pin budget is zero. The catalog currently excludes offload as host-only, so this row is optional. |
| Qwen3-VL CUDA-IPC multimodal feature borrowing + Hopper serving defaults | absence of pixel-value H2D copies (`CudaIpcTensorTransportProxy.borrow_on_target_device`); `Expanding multimodal decode CUDA graph max_bs ...` log | `python/sglang/srt/models/qwen3_vl.py::_materialize_visual_items`<br>`python/sglang/srt/multimodal/transport/cuda_ipc.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/arg_groups/model_overrides/qwen3_vl.py` | On ≥60 GB Hopper Qwen3-VL(-MoE), the resolver sets `mm_feature_transport=cuda_ipc`, `prefill_decode_interval=22`, decode attention `flashinfer`, `radix_eviction_policy=priority`, VLM caches 0, and decode graph `max_bs` up to `max_running_requests`. Pixel values are borrowed zero-copy for single-consumer items.<br>Evidence: `#36411` merged 2026-09-12 (`0a57403468`): 57.09 → 68.55 req/s, median TTFT 2.62 → 1.31 s (1×H100, concurrency 373, 1,200 unique images; PR table). The body also lists FA3 prefill and decode graph max bs 400 as resolved defaults | Qwen3-VL H100 traces on new builds show long decode runs between prefills (interval 22) and no feature H2D. Do not treat the prefill/decode cadence as a scheduler bug. |
| SGLang per-phase CUDA graph backends (`full` / `breakable` / `tc_piecewise` / `disabled`), incl. prefill graphs | `--cuda-graph-backend-decode` / `--cuda-graph-backend-prefill`<br>`refresh_for_breakable_cuda_graph_replay_`<br>`prefer_eager_mixed_prefill_under_dp_attention`<br>`_process_output_after_replay` | `python/sglang/srt/model_executor/cuda_graph_config.py::Backend`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner_backend/breakable_cuda_graph_backend.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner/prefill_cuda_graph_runner.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner/decode_cuda_graph_runner.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/arg_groups/cuda_graph_hook.py` | In this slice: ROCm DSV4 breakable prefill graphs (#37810) removed an `.item()` sync in the metadata pad, and keep MIXED prefill eager under DP attention. `--cuda-graph-prefill-max-context` / `--context-bucket` (#36534) caps context-shaped metadata and indexer logits, and larger contexts fall back to eager. A decode-graph eager logits tail runs after replay inside the timer (#40222). Out-of-tree platforms can supply full graph backends (#37969).<br>Evidence: `#37810` merged 2026-09-16 (`a813224e78`)<br>`#36534` merged 2026-09-15 (`c9a8fba991`)<br>`#40222` merged 2026-09-18 (`111aeedd37`)<br>`#37969` merged 2026-09-18 (`1fdd6c8921`)<br>`#39574` merged 2026-09-15 (padded-row / input_embeds replay fixes) | Eager prefill kernels for long contexts, or MIXED batches under DP attention, are intentional fallbacks. Kernels after a graph launch can be the eager logits tail. The catalog has no SGLang graph-mode row yet. |
| SGLang GPU→CPU sync / host-gap removal (mainline, this slice) | `nonzero` / boolean-mask indexing<br>`.item()` / `.tolist()`<br>`torch.all(...)` before `npu_top_k_top_p`<br>`index_select` / `index_copy_`<br>`async_d2h` | `python/sglang/srt/layers/sampler.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/sampling/sampling_batch_info.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/managers/mm_utils.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/disaggregation/utils.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/hardware_backend/npu/attention/ascend_backend.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/hardware_backend/npu/graph_runner/npu_graph_runner.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/logprob_processor.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/deepseek_v4_backend_hip_radix.py` | Custom logit processors keep host rows plus cached device indices (#39234; bs1 B200 ITL 2.41→1.35 ms). NPU top-k/top-p eligibility is a host flag (#39404). MM placeholder ids use a pinned async H2D (#40010). Text-only requests skip VMM error gathers (#40005). Singleton PD transfer-status all-reduces are skipped (#40003). Non-DFlash NPU replay skips the `seq_lens` D2H (#39879). The AMD DSV4 draft skips compressed-KV metadata (#40205). Input top-logprobs use async pinned D2H with `input_copy_done` (#40038, #40007).<br>Evidence: `#39234` merged 2026-09-18 (`8ea0ee300d`); `#39404` 2026-09-16; `#40010` 2026-09-18; `#40005` 2026-09-18; `#40003` 2026-09-18; `#39879` 2026-09-18; `#40205` 2026-09-19; `#40038` 2026-09-19; `#40007` 2026-09-18 (all merged) | CPU gaps with tiny GPU kernels in these paths on a post-slice build are a regression. On an older build they are this known family. Compare with the vLLM sync-removal row (§17). |
| CUDA-graph replay stream explosion from fork ordering and ProcessGroupNCCL inside capture | Dozens of graph-replay stream rows for one forward (96 for the K3 target, 7 for the DSpark draft)<br>`_bfa_alt_stream`<br>`_gate_pending_stream`<br>`ncclDevKernel_Broadcast` | `python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py` / `_compute_output_gate`<br>`python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py` (KDA `fused_qkvg_proj` recorded before the side-stream GEMVs)<br>`python/sglang/srt/distributed/parallel_state.py` | Inside capture, the main-branch kernels are recorded before the auxiliary-stream kernels, and the MLA gate GEMM is recorded at the o_proj boundary (fork early, record late). Captured broadcasts go through PyNccl on the capture stream (CUDA as well as HIP).<br>Evidence: #40640 merged (`c4d3770a68`, 2026-09-21/22); #40658 merged 2026-09-21 (`bc22e1de9e`) | Many replay streams are a capture-ordering artifact that adds cross-stream dependencies; fix it by capture order, not by deleting the overlap. The PRs measured K3 target streams 96→4 and DSpark draft 7→1, with BS1 +1.03% on TP8. Use with the `torch-profiler-layer-track` skill when a trace has hundreds of graph stream rows. |
| PP + speculative relay: launch extend microbatches before the spec output exchange | Per-stage idle gaps between PP microbatch forwards; stage utilization capped at (pp−1)/pp | `python/sglang/srt/managers/scheduler_pp_mixin.py::_pp_exchange_outputs_before_forward` | With `pp_async_batch_depth=0` and a spec relay, non-last stages used to exchange outputs before launching even for extend microbatches. Now only verify rounds exchange first, because the tree rebuild needs it to avoid deadlock.<br>Evidence: #40499 merged 2026-09-21 (`0229025127`) | A PP+spec trace whose per-stage step period ≈ `sum(stage time)/(pp−1)` points to this ordering. The PR measured Qwen3.5-397B PP4 +21–24%, DSV4-Flash TP1×PP4 +17%, GLM-5.3-Flash +35%, K3+DSpark +30%, and TP2×PP2 +70–107%. |
| Implicit device→host syncs removed from metadata prep (GPU idle before the next launch) | `aten::repeat_interleave` without `output_size`<br>`aten::item` / `.tolist()` / `.any()` on GPU tensors<br>`cudaStreamSynchronize` gaps in prefill metadata | `python/sglang/srt/speculative/dspark_components/dspark_worker_v2.py` (`output_size=num_tokens`)<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/dsa/kpool_plan.py` (`req_pool_indices_cpu`, `output_size`)<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/hybrid_linear_attn_backend.py::_has_cpu_prefill_track_metadata`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/mamba/prefill_track_metadata.py::build_prefill_track_plan`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/qsa/sparse_attn.py::sparse_gqa_packed_decode_triton`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/aiter_backend.py::_asm_context_prefill_indices`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/flashinfer_backend.py` | Lengths are computed from CPU mirrors (`seq_lens_cpu`, `extend_*_cpu`, `req_pool_indices_cpu`) or passed as `output_size`. The AITER ASM context-prefill gather indices are built once per batch instead of per layer, and the ROCm QSA decode avoids the `.item()` that blocked graph capture.<br>Evidence: #40111 merged 2026-09-22; #39695 merged 2026-09-19; #39688 merged 2026-09-19; #38876 merged 2026-09-23; #39901 merged 2026-09-22; #40796 merged 2026-09-23; #36187 merged 2026-09-21 (NPU) | A GPU gap right after `repeat_interleave`, `nonzero`, `.item()` or `.tolist()` in metadata prep is this family. Check whether the CPU mirror reached `ForwardBatch`; `req_pool_indices_cpu` is only fresh for non-speculative extend. |
| Pageable H2D copies of sampling metadata made pinned + non-blocking | `Memcpy HtoD (Pageable -> Device)`<br>blocking `cudaMemcpyAsync` before the sampler | `python/sglang/srt/sampling/sampling_batch_info.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/sampling/penaltylib/min_new_tokens.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/logprob_processor.py::get_token_ids_logprobs_raw`<br>Kernel/caller attribution: `python/sglang/kernels/aot/csrc/kvcacheio/transfer.cu` | Penalty params, logit bias, masks, stop-token IDs (padded once on CPU) and token-ID logprob indices are staged in pinned memory and copied with `non_blocking=True`.<br>Evidence: #39777 merged 2026-09-20 (`f31a7bd45c`) | Pageable H2D copies serializing the sampler or penalizer in decode traces can mean an older build. Also check the frozen-head backend, shape, graph/capture and fallback guards before attributing the split to an old version. |
| Triton re-specialization on dynamic sequence length (JIT compile stalls per new length) | First-seen-length host stall in `apply_interleaved_rope_kernel` | `python/sglang/kernels/ops/attention/mrope.py::apply_interleaved_rope_kernel` (`do_not_specialize=["S"]`) | `S` is no longer a constexpr, so one compiled variant serves all lengths.<br>Evidence: #40371 merged 2026-09-22 (`c53cc8e1eb`) | Multi-second CPU gaps on new VLM prompt lengths came from Triton recompiles. Check `do_not_specialize` before blaming the scheduler. |
| Elastic EP: decode full CUDA graphs recaptured after scale-up | Graph recapture after an EP membership change<br>`ElasticCudaGraphConfig` | `python/sglang/srt/model_executor/model_runner_components/cuda_graph_setup.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/elastic_ep/elastic_ep.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/arg_groups/parallel_hook.py` | Elastic EP previously required decode and prefill graphs disabled. Decode `full` graphs are now allowed and recaptured after scale-up. Prefill graphs, speculative decoding, dLLM and PDMux are still rejected.<br>Evidence: #33723 merged 2026-09-23 (`de123f38bb`) | Eager decode under elastic EP is no longer mandatory, so a missing decode graph is a config choice. |
| NPU batch-invariant FIA made graph-capturable | `npu_fused_infer_attention_score_batch_invariant` (`.out`)<br>`register_npu_graph_handler` | `python/sglang/srt/hardware_backend/npu/batch_invariant_ops/npu_batch_invariant_ops.py` | Deterministic mode on NPU no longer forces eager attention.<br>Evidence: #39607 merged 2026-09-24 (`03fcbe147f`) | NPU traces with deterministic inference can now show graph replay. |
| CUDA-graph replay input staging: fused small copies | `_small_copy_kernel` (one launch per phase) replacing many `copy_` / `Memcpy DtoD` | `python/sglang/kernels/ops/memory/small_copy.py::try_small_copy`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/cuda_graph_buffer_registry.py::_grouped_foreach_copy_`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner_utils/buffers.py::_grouped_foreach_copy_` | Generic (all models), not Qwen-only. Up to N tensors of <=8192 elements, 1-D/2-D, same or int32<->int64 dtype, non-aliasing, are copied in one Triton launch before replay. Otherwise grouped `foreach_copy_` is used<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#41166 merged 2026-09-27 (`aa7a976807`) | A burst of tiny copy kernels right before each decode graph replay can mean fallback (large or aliasing buffers). This is an existing host-gap reduction |
| DP-attention speculative/prefill coordination | decode ranks keep running draft + verify while another DP rank prefills<br>`dp_spec_prefill_coordination_applied`<br>eager (non-graph) steps on coordinated iterations | `python/sglang/srt/speculative/dp_spec_prefill_coordination.py::DPSpecPrefillCoordinationPlan`<br>`python/sglang/srt/speculative/eagle_worker_v2.py` (`_forward_dp_spec_prefill_coordination`)<br>`python/sglang/srt/speculative/dspark_components/dspark_worker_v2.py` | `SGLANG_ENABLE_DP_SPEC_PREFILL_COORDINATION=1` (default off). Requires single-layer EAGLE/EAGLE3 (DSpark added by #41623). Each rank keeps its own forward mode with SUM_LEN padding. Coordinated steps set `can_run_decode_cuda_graph=False` and `can_run_dp_prefill_cuda_graph=False`<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#40118 merged 2026-09-24 (`1417345f5f`); fixes #41096, #41162, #41179 merged 2026-09-24; DSpark #41623 merged 2026-09-29 (`d3f8a8f4f5`) | With the env on, expect mixed prefill/verify across ranks and eager kernels on those steps, without spec decoding being disabled globally. Without it, decode ranks idle or drop spec during peer prefill |
| LLM breakable / prefill CUDA-graph backends (absent from catalogs; touched by this slice) | `cuda_graph_backend_prefill=breakable`<br>`tc_piecewise`<br>`full`<br>eager islands between graph segments | `python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner_backend/breakable_cuda_graph_backend.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner/prefill_cuda_graph_runner.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/dsa/kpool_prefill_cuda_graph.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/arg_groups/fields/exec_.py` (`cuda_graph_config`) | Per-phase graph backends (base refactor #23906, 2026-06-09). In this slice: DSA pooled-indexer breakable prefill bridge under DP attention (#41311); DP-rank graph eligibility synchronization for EAGLE PD (#32196); K3/DFlash aux-hidden-state buffer shared across decode graph sizes (#40159); fused small staging copies (#41166)<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#41311 merged 2026-09-25 (`4bb645b8e2`), #32196 merged 2026-09-28 (`7889fbab9a`), #40159 merged 2026-09-29 (`84523d6785`) | Prefill traces may show graph-replayed segments separated by eager breaks. Rank-asymmetric eager fallback before #32196 was a bug, not a kernel gap. Check `--cuda-graph-backend-{decode,prefill}` and `--cuda-graph-max-seq-len-prefill` before attributing launch gaps |
| Diffusion breakable CUDA graph coverage: LongCat-Image-Edit-Turbo | BCG replay segments in DiT denoise | `python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/server_args/server_args.py` (`BREAKABLE_CUDA_GRAPH_SUPPORTED_MODEL_IDS`)<br>`python/sglang/multimodal_gen/runtime/breakable_cuda_graph/model_padders/longcat_image.py` | The model is added to the BCG allow-list. Warmup synthesizes an input image of the requested resolution so the captured graph matches the real VL-prefix length<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#40384 merged 2026-09-24 | If LongCat Edit-Turbo traces are fully eager, check the model id and warmup resolutions |
| NPU graph parameter update overlapped with replay | `graph.update` (two-stage ACL) running concurrently with `graph.replay` | `python/sglang/srt/hardware_backend/npu/graph_runner/npu_cudagraph_backend.py` | The update future is awaited after `graph.replay()` instead of before, so the CPU-side update overlaps device execution. #40446 adds a rebind fence so host `seq_lens` is not rewritten while the device still reads it<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#40814 merged 2026-09-28 (`6cbdfec453`); sgl-project/sglang#40446 merged 2026-09-29 (`6624999385`) | On Ascend, a host gap equal to the update time before every replay is the pre-#40814 serialization |
| Breakable prefill CUDA graph (BCG) for QSA / Qwen4-Exp, with eager breaks for the host-dependent indexer | graph replay with eager-break windows around `_breakable_qsa_indexer` and `_breakable_prepare_ple_batch`<br>no per-layer `req_pool_indices.tolist()` D2H on replay | `python/sglang/srt/models/qwen4_exp.py::_breakable_qsa_indexer`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner_backend_utils/breakable_cuda_graph/breakable_cuda_graph.py::eager_on_graph`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner/prefill_cuda_graph_runner.py`<br>`python/sglang/srt/model_executor/runner_backend/breakable_cuda_graph_backend.py` | `Qwen4ExpForConditionalGeneration` (text-only) joined the BCG-supported archs. QSA prefill metadata carries host lengths, and replay reuses `token_slot_table`. The HC stream is returned through a side channel; the MTP draft takes it as `inputs_embeds`. Multimodal batches fall back to eager. Related coverage: #41154 lets the EAGLE3 draft BCG capture `input_embeds` for multimodal targets (Qwen3.5/VL); #41837 removes the GLM-5.3-Flash BCG `chunked_prefill_size=4096` pin.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#41729 merged 2026-10-03 (`8ff02f5820`); #41154 merged 2026-09-30; #41837 merged 2026-10-01 | Prefill launch gaps on Qwen3.8 Flash-Next can mean BCG is off or the batch has MM input. Missing graph windows on an EAGLE3 draft for a VL target can mean a build before #41154. |
| HIP DSpark draft-block attention metadata built inside the draft CUDA graph | fewer eager metadata kernels / H2D copies before each DSpark draft replay on gfx950 | `python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/deepseek_v4_backend_hip_radix.py::DSV4RawDSparkDraftMetadata` | The graph records raw `req_pool_indices`/`seq_lens`/`out_cache_loc` and runs `init_forward_metadata_dspark_draft_block` in-graph. `SGLANG_HIP_DSPARK_DRAFT_RAW_METADATA` defaults on for HIP.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#42014 merged 2026-10-01 (`f45c004e18`) | Eager DSpark draft metadata between replays on MI355X can mean the env is off or the build is older. Also check the frozen-head backend, shape, graph/capture and fallback guards before attributing the split to an old version. |
| PD decode: prebuilt last-token H2D made non-blocking under the overlap scheduler | removes a blocking HtoD copy on the schedule stream that waited for the in-flight forward (GPU idle bubble) | `python/sglang/srt/disaggregation/decode_schedule_batch_mixin.py` | Pinned CPU tensor plus `.to(device, non_blocking=True)`.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#42113 merged 2026-10-02 (`7dd93b01a5`) | A PD-decode GPU gap aligned with a pageable HtoD copy can mean an older build. |
| PD prefill continuous input polling (opt-in, host side) | shorter idle between prefill forwards when bootstrap/transfer threads starve; at TP=1, 100 us GIL-yield sleeps | `python/sglang/srt/disaggregation/prefill.py` (`PREFILL_INPUT_POLL_INTERVAL_S`, `_is_continuous_input_polling_enabled`) | `SGLANG_ENABLE_DISAGG_PREFILL_CONTINUOUS_INPUT_POLLING` (default False) keeps ingesting while a prefill result is pending, with at most 2 forwards in flight. Requires attention DP=CP=1, non-speculative, generation.<br>Evidence: sgl-project/sglang#42035 merged 2026-10-03 (`6acf2f4736`) | Host-only scheduler change: use it to explain prefill idle gaps, not as a kernel fusion. |
| TRT-LLM LoRA CUDA-graph specialization: separate base-only and LoRA graph sets | `CUDAGraphRunner` keys with `use_lora_graph`<br>absence of dummy LoRA GEMMs in base-only batches | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/cuda_graph_runner.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/model_engine.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/peft/lora/config.py` (`cuda_graph_specialize_lora`) | Opt-in (default False) and incompatible with attention-DP. Without it, every graph replays all LoRA ops for all requests using dummies.<br>Evidence: `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#17412` merged 2026-08-25 (`16c32cce57`) | With LoRA enabled but few LoRA requests, dummy LoRA grouped GEMMs in every decode graph are expected unless `cuda_graph_specialize_lora=True`. Expect longer warmup and more graph memory when it is on. |
| TRT-LLM DSA cross-layer indexer-share: index remap batched into one grouped launch per indexer group | `convertReqIndexToGlobalGroupedKernel`<br>`trtllm::convert_req_index_to_global_grouped`<br>(per-layer: `convertReqIndexToGlobalKernel`) | `cpp/tensorrt_llm/kernels/convertReqIndexToGlobal.cu`<br>`cpp/tensorrt_llm/thop/convertReqIndexToGlobalOp.cpp`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/attention/backends/sparse/dsa/backend.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/attention/backends/sparse/dsa/indexer.py` | On by default for generation forwards (`TRTLLM_DISABLE_DSA_GROUP_REMAP=1` disables it). Inert without shared indexer layers. The leader computes the whole group with `grid.z` = group size. Context and draft passes stay per-layer.<br>Evidence: `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18391` merged 2026-08-31 (`4593e50d78`) | The PR measured GLM-5.2 TP8 low-concurrency decode on GB300: 78 to 24 launches per step, ~187 to ~61 µs, about 1.6% of a decode step. Per-layer remap launches in generation can mean the env is set or the model has no shared layers. |
| TRT-LLM feature-mode encoder CUDA graphs for fixed-shape encoders (Whisper) | `EncoderCUDAGraphRunner` replays<br>H2D feature copies outside the graph | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/cuda_graph_runner.py::EncoderCUDAGraphRunner`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/engine/runners/encoder.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/models/modeling_whisper.py::encoder_graph_spec` | The graph key is batch size only. Shapes come from the model's `encoder_graph_spec()`. Dedicated streams and pools, a padding limit of 12.5%, and eager fallback otherwise. Extends the packed-token encoder graphs from #16706.<br>Evidence: `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#17030` merged 2026-08-31 (`19356d5ee0`) | A launch-bound eager Whisper encoder can mean `encoder_cuda_graph_config` is unset or batches exceed the padding limit. |
| TRT-LLM MiniMax-M3 MSA inter-iteration idle reduction: host-built page table and `out_cache_loc`, pinned non-blocking copies, on-device plan-length patch, prefill-first mixed-batch split | GPU idle gaps between MSA steps<br>`cudaMemcpyAsync` (pinned)<br>removed `.item()` syncs<br>`_mixed_batch_split` | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/attention/backends/sparse/minimax_m3/msa_backend.py::on_update_kv_lens`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/attention/backends/sparse/minimax_m3/common.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/attention/backends/sparse/minimax_m3/cache_manager.py`<br>`3rdparty/patches/msa_strided_paged_kv.patch` | Removes per-request device kernels and per-token `.item()` reads. Mixed batches now split context rows (sparse GQA prefill) from generation rows (paged decode) instead of planning the whole batch as sparse prefill.<br>Evidence: `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#17986` merged 2026-08-26 (`a2888334f9`) | The PR reports TP4, ISL 8192, OSL 128, c=320 can mean request latency of 69.3 to 57.9 s (host-gap part) and 105.7 to 52.5 s (mixed-batch split). Large inter-step GPU idle or a whole mixed batch in the prefill kernel can mean pre-#17986. |
| TRT-LLM executor per-iteration host overhead: hang-detector heartbeat as a deadline store, timing events only when read, response GPU timings computed once per batch | GPU idle between iterations in host-bound workloads<br>(removed per-iteration `call_soon_threadsafe` wakeups and unread `torch.cuda.Event.record`)<br>`cudaEventElapsedTime` once per batch | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/hang_detector.py::HangDetector`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/py_executor.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/perf_metrics_manager.py::compute_batch_gpu_times` | `checkpoint()` becomes one clock read plus a float store. The start-event record is gated on `print_log or enable_iter_perf_stats`. Per-request elapsed-time queries are cached per batch.<br>Evidence: `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#17531` merged 2026-08-27 (`767af6f285`)<br>`#18053` merged 2026-08-26 (`428839007a`) | The PR measured whisper-tiny on SM120 at +18.4% bs8 and +11.9% bs32. In host-bound small models, look for inter-iteration CPU gaps before blaming kernels. Enabling `enable_iter_perf_stats` adds event records back. |
| TRT-LLM disaggregated idle-loop poll fix: no full poll-interval sleep without in-flight KV sessions | executor-loop idle of one `kv_transfer_sender_future_timeout_ms` per iteration on context servers | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/disaggregation/transceiver.py` (`wait_num = min(wait_num, len(sessions))`) | Clamps the wait target to in-flight sessions, so `check_context_transfer_status(1)` with nothing in flight returns immediately.<br>Evidence: `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18175` merged 2026-08-31 (`f787e8a1b6`) | Periodic idle gaps on an idle disagg context server, delaying admission of new requests, can mean pre-#18175. |
| TRT-LLM first-use JIT/autotune stall avoidance: CuteDSL MLA decode autotuner falls back to bucket-aligned `split_kv`, and trtllm-gen sparse FMHA warmup mesh densified | host stall plus `cute.compile` inside the serving loop at the first unseen batch size<br>trtllm-gen FMHA warmup over `seqLenQ ∈ {1,2,4,8,32,64,128}` | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/custom_ops/cute_dsl_custom_ops.py` (CuteDSL MLA decode runner)<br>`cpp/tensorrt_llm/kernels/trtllmGenKernels/fmha/fmhaKernels.h` (`kDefaultWarmupSeqLenQCandidates`) | The fallback rounds the batch down to its tuning bucket (`last_positive_power_of_2`) so an already-compiled `split_kv` variant is reused. Sparse-attention warmup covers small Q lengths.<br>Evidence: `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#17792` merged 2026-08-26 (`9fc603baa0`)<br>`#17961` merged 2026-08-26 (`3e2749fccf`) | A multi-millisecond CPU gap on the first decode at a new batch size is a JIT miss. These PRs remove the common cases, so a remaining miss is a coverage bug. |
| TRT-LLM speculative-decoding CUDA graphs with advanced (non-greedy) sampling: capture via synthetic non-greedy dummy requests, and the group-greedy graph key refreshed per iteration | CUDA-graph replays containing in-graph top-k/top-p sampling kernels<br>`group_all_greedy_sample` graph-key selection | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/model_engine.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/speculative/interface.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/resource_manager.py` | Replaces the mutable `_force_non_greedy_for_capture` flag. That flag once leaked into replay and rewrote every request to temperature 0.7 / top-k 50 / top-p 0.9 (fixed by #17033). A stale group-greedy decision could pick the wrong graph (#17710).<br>Evidence: `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#17936` merged 2026-08-27 (`710fd258cb`)<br>`#17710` merged 2026-08-24 (`c82fabff54`) | If a spec-decode trace shows the greedy graph for non-greedy requests, or the reverse, check graph-key selection before kernel timing. |
| TRT-LLM attention-DP per-iteration host collectives: merged and buffer-based int64 | GPU idle gap at the start of each ADP iteration (host MPI allgathers)<br>`tp_allgather_int64` / `tp_cp_allgather_int64` / `broadcast_int64`<br>`small_payload=True` | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/distributed/communicator.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/model_engine.py::_get_all_rank_num_tokens_and_spec_counts`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/cuda_graph_runner.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/py_executor.py` | Attention and spec counts are exchanged in one allgather. The context-request-count allgather runs only when prefill CUDA graphs are on, and a redundant re-gather was dropped (#18293). The remaining per-iteration exchanges use a single `MPI.Allgather` or `Bcast` on int64 numpy buffers, with no pickle and no length exchange (#18639).<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18293 merged 2026-09-01<br>NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18639 merged 2026-09-04 | Host-side gaps before `forward` under attention DP are a known reduced cost. Measure them in nsys CPU rows before proposing comm changes. |
| TRT-LLM ADP idle-rank dummy context request shrunk to 1 token | Previously: a full `max_num_tokens` prefill on idle ADP ranks (disagg with transceiver)<br>iteration log fields `adp_dummy_ctx_tokens` / `adp_dummy_gen_tokens` | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/py_executor.py::_compute_adp_dummy_tokens` | The CTX-role dummy now uses `token_nums=[1]` (Helix raises it to 2). Iteration stats count dummy tokens.<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18308 merged 2026-09-02 (also #18157 refused-dummy handling 2026-09-04) | On older builds a huge prefill on an idle ADP rank was dummy work. In new traces check the new iteration-log fields. |
| TRT-LLM FMHA library selection cache (per attention layer) | Python time between kernels in eager attention<br>`FmhaManager`<br>`TRTLLM_FMHA_CACHE_SANITY_CHECK` | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/attention/backends/fmha/manager.py::FmhaManager`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/attention/backends/trtllm.py` | Caches `is_supported` selection per (batch-size grid bucket, gen Q length, mask type, spec state, output dtype). It is bypassed during autotune and cleared on a library rebuild. It was extracted into `FmhaManager` (#18548).<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18350 merged 2026-09-02 UTC (B200 TinyLlama eager: -2.62% mean request latency, 11.147 to 10.855 ms/step)<br>#18548 merged 2026-09-04 | A large eager per-layer host overhead in TrtllmAttention can mean an old build or many distinct cache keys. |
| TRT-LLM FlashInfer decode plan keying (plan reuse / bounded wrapper cache) | `plan()` host calls per step<br>`BatchDecodeWithPagedKVCacheWrapper` | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/attention/backends/flashinfer.py::PlanParams` | #18596 dropped `num_generations` from single-token decode plan keys, which reuses plans. #18557 removed `num_generations` from the key entirely: eager metadata **replans its shared wrappers every `prepare()`**, while CUDA-graph metadata keeps private wrapper caches per captured batch size.<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18596 merged 2026-09-04, superseded by NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18557 merged 2026-09-09 | In eager FlashInfer decode, expect a `plan` on every step by design. A memory blow-up from per-batch-size wrappers is fixed after #18557. |
| TRT-LLM sync-free metadata preparation (KDA prefill chunk indices; GVR prior from device lengths) | Removed: `cudaStreamSynchronize` / D2H for `repeat_interleave` sizing; synchronous H2D of host `seq_lens` per layer | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/modules/kimi_kda/kimi_k3_mamba_metadata.py::KimiK3MambaMetadata`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/modules/top_k.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/attention/backends/sparse/dsa/indexer.py` | K3 KDA chunk indices are computed once per step by a `torch.compile`d helper. `repeat_interleave(..., output_size=)` avoids a device read (#18064). The GVR prior gather uses `seq_lens_cuda` (#18646).<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18064 merged 2026-09-02<br>NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18646 merged 2026-09-04 | Per-layer small H2D/D2H syncs in K3 or DSA traces point to old builds or a new regression. Compare vLLM §17 sync-removal family. |
| TRT-LLM in-graph sampling (temperature/top-k/top-p captured into the decode CUDA graph) | FlashInfer sampling kernels inside graph replay (`sample_from_logits_op`: `top_k_mask_logits_op`, `softmax_op`, `top_p_sampling_from_probs_op`)<br>`SampleType.FAST` / `SampleType.FULL` | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/cuda_graph_runner.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/sampler/sampler.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/sampler/ops/flashinfer.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/llmapi/llm_args.py` (`TorchLlmArgs.enable_in_graph_sampling`) | Opt-in (prototype, default False). FAST-tier batches sample inside the graph, using per-row Philox seed/offset with no host sync. Others (including greedy and min_p) stay eager. The tier is part of the graph key and of the ADP graph-consensus exchange. An extra graph set is captured, which costs warmup time and memory. Seeded requests draw different tokens than the eager path.<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18437 merged 2026-09-08 (Qwen2-0.5B bs=4 H200: FAST tier 113.3 to 84.5 ms, -25.4%) | Eager sampler kernels after the forward are the default. When the flag is on, the absence of sampler launches after replay is expected. Note: the PR body calls the flag `enable_fast_sampler`, but the merged field is `enable_in_graph_sampling`. |
| TRT-LLM MoE-LoRA CUDA-graph parameter tables (allocation-free per-step host path) | Host time before graph replay with MoE LoRA | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/peft/lora/cuda_graph_lora_params.py` | Per-(layer, module) pinned rank and pointer tables are refreshed in place with no temporaries. Ranks are masked by pointer validity (correctness).<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18527 merged 2026-09-09 | Small host-gap and correctness fix. LoRA-MoE graph replay host cost should be flat. |
| TRT-LLM breakable prefill CUDA graph (BCG) for hybrid Mamba2 / Nemotron-H: eager boundary custom op plus in-place SSM-update registration | `trtllm::mamba2_custom_op_inplace` (eager node inside the breakable graph)<br>`trtllm::flashinfer_selective_state_update`<br>absent: per-layer functionalization `clone`/`copy_` of the SSM state cache | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/modules/mamba/mamba2_mixer.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/compilation/piecewise_optimizer.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/compilation/utils.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/breakable_cuda_graph/breakable_cuda_graph.py::eager_on_graph` | The whole conv+SSM core runs as one opaque eager node (the counterpart of `gdn_custom_op_inplace`), so the surrounding pieces stay uniform in `num_tokens` and are graph-captured. `flashinfer_selective_state_update` is registered as mutating `(state, out)`, so the inplace-copy rewrite removes the full SSM-state clone from decode graphs.<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18888 merged 2026-09-14 | For Nemotron-H prefill with BCG:<br>• Graphed segments between eager Mamba2 nodes are the intended shape.<br>• Under compile, a large per-layer clone of SSM state in decode graphs can mean a pre-#18888 build.<br>• Recompile-limit failures (batch-composition specialization) were the problem this fixed. |
| TRT-LLM fused DSA decode metadata (on by default): about 30 eager pointwise/scan launches become one Triton launch | `_fused_dsa_decode_metadata_kernel` (`fused_dsa_decode_metadata`)<br>absent: `cumsum` / `searchsorted` / floor-div / `remainder` / clamp ladder in `on_update_kv_lens` | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/attention/backends/sparse/dsa/kernels.py::fused_dsa_decode_metadata`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/attention/backends/sparse/dsa/metadata.py` | For pure decode steps (`num_contexts == 0`), one launch computes `req_idx_per_token`, the FP8 and scale slot mappings, and the generation indptrs. In #19094 the old `fused_metadata.py` was folded into `kernels.py`, and the opt-in `TRTLLM_FUSED_DSA_METADATA` became the opt-out `TRTLLM_DISABLE_FUSED_DSA_METADATA`. Mixed steps keep the eager chain.<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#19094 merged 2026-09-17. The PR shows parity: 152 exact metadata tests, and GPQA/AIME deltas within ±2 on DS-V3.2 and GLM-5.2. | In DS-V3.2 / GLM-5.2 DSA decode traces, a burst of tiny integer kernels before the indexer can mean a mixed step, the disable env, or a pre-2026-09-17 build. Their absence is the default. Also check the frozen-head backend, shape, graph/capture and fallback guards before attributing the split to an old version. |
| TRT-LLM MiniMax-M3 Triton-backend metadata: per-token KV slots derived on device (no `.item()` host loops) | `index_select` / `repeat_interleave` on device in metadata prepare<br>absent: host `tolist()`/`.item()` stalls in `prepare()` | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/attention/backends/sparse/minimax_m3/triton_metadata.py` (`derive_q_positions_and_cache_slots`, `derive_decode_cache_slots`) | `out_cache_loc`, `q_positions` and `q_batch_row` are computed on device from `req_to_token`, clamped to the page table. This also makes the overlap scheduler's optimistic lengths safe.<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18872 merged 2026-09-15 (Eagle3 for MiniMax-M3) | CPU gaps before MiniMax-M3 Triton-backend steps caused by per-token `.item()` reads were removed. A remaining host gap there is something else. |
| TRT-LLM DFlash and Mamba-cache host-sync removal: pinned staging plus one async scatter, `inplace_slice_copy`, `index_fill_` | H2D `cudaMemcpyAsync` from pinned memory instead of pageable copies<br>`inplace_slice_copy` instead of a functionalized buffer clone | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/speculative/dflash.py` (`_write_ctx_len`)<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/kv_cache/mamba_cache_manager.py` | Acceptance and context-length bookkeeping is batched into one pinned-staged async scatter. Mamba `cuda_state_indices` and dummy masks are copied through pinned staging. Advanced-index zero assignments became `index_fill_`.<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18553 merged 2026-09-12 | In the DFlash pre-draft region, a pageable `cudaMemcpy` sync or per-request tiny H2D copies point to a pre-#18553 build (PR: +4.2% from this alone). Also check the frozen-head backend, shape, graph/capture and fallback guards before attributing the split to an old version. |
| TRT-LLM host CPU-contention reduction: NUMA affinity applied to all threads, NumExpr pinned to one thread | none on the GPU; visible as smaller and less jittery CPU launch gaps | `tensorrt_llm/llmapi/utils.py::_reapply_current_thread_affinity_to_all_threads`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/py_executor.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_bootstrap.py::_set_numexpr_thread_default` | The affinity mask is applied to every TID in `/proc/self/task` and reapplied right before the executor threads start; opt out with `TLLM_NUMA_AWARE_WORKER_AFFINITY=0`. `NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS` defaults to 1 and numexpr is imported lazily.<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18701 merged 2026-09-10; #18556 merged 2026-09-16 | If host gaps between kernels vary from run to run under TP/EP with MPI, check affinity before blaming the scheduler. Builds without #18701 and #18556 can oversubscribe cores. |
| TRT-LLM KV-cache events streaming publisher (ZMQ PUB from a background thread; no rank-0 gather or per-iteration pull) | host only | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/kv_cache_events.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/llmapi/llm_args.py` (`KVEventsConfig`) | `kv_cache_config.kv_events_config` (prototype) has each rank encode and publish its own events. It requires KVCacheManagerV2 on its Python backend. The buffered `/kv_cache_events` pull path remains the default.<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#17023 merged 2026-09-09 | Host-only. | TRT-LLM KVCacheManagerV2 scheduler: admit one context per uncached prefix, so duplicates wait one iteration for the reuse hit | host scheduler; visible as fewer duplicate prefill tokens | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/scheduler/scheduler_v2.py` (`_is_prefix_skip_candidate`, `_skip_pays_off_under_reuse_policy`) | When several requests share an uncached prefix, only one is admitted for prefill. The others are deferred one iteration and then hit the reused blocks.<br>Evidence: NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#18195 merged 2026-09-10 | Do not count duplicate-prefix prefill GPU time as waste on builds with #18195. A one-iteration TTFT bump on duplicate prompts is expected. |
| Runtime JIT stalls removed: DeepGEMM JIT warmup buckets, and KDA prefill warmed for both chunk-aligned and ragged lengths | seconds-long GPU idle on the first request (JIT compile) **disappears** | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/utils.py::deep_gemm_jit_warmup_buckets`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/model_engine.py::_apply_chunk_alignment`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/modules/kimi_kda/kimi_k3_mamba_metadata.py` | The DeepGEMM tuning config uses warmup buckets and `exclude_from_cache=True`.
