Expected to be gone after #18813. Exclude warmup iterations from traces anyway. |
| Host-gap reductions visible as shorter CPU→GPU gaps: sampled iteration stats, scheduler prefix-probe skip, BOLT-optimized aarch64 wheel, frontend tokenization | (no kernel names; smaller host gaps between `forward` steps)<br>`iter_perf_stats_interval`<br>`enable_tokenization_cache` | `tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/py_executor.py::_init_iter_stats_if_sampled`<br>`tensorrt_llm/_torch/pyexecutor/scheduler/scheduler_v2.py::_has_context_chunk_budget`<br>`scripts/bolt/apply_bolt.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/inputs/prefix_token_cache.py`<br>`tensorrt_llm/executor/request.py` | `enable_iter_perf_stats` overhead is reduced by `iter_perf_stats_interval: N`. ROCm launch-overhead comparisons must record MRV1/MRV2 and the breakable state. On CUDA, breakable models run without torch.compile, so compile-pass fusions cannot appear there |

A pageable H2D before a GPU bubble is a known vLLM family. `VLLM_GPU_SYNC_CHECK` (see D) flags these copies |

Before #55404 each dummy step drained the stream on hybrid (GDN / short-conv / KDA) models |

Host gaps between draft steps under DP predate #54856 |

PCP batches keep the old behaviour. Mixed FULL graphs capture with `max_query_len=num_tokens`, so the prefill kernel variant is captured (#58275).<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#58400 merged 2026-09-28 (`e7c903609f`). vllm-project/vllm#58275 merged 2026-09-24 (`23110a0c9e`). | On MRV2 decode-node traces, intermittent PIECEWISE steps when requests arrive can mean an image before #58400, or PCP. |
| Encoder (ViT) CUDA graphs: new model plus host-sync-free replay metadata | `cudagraph_mm_encoder` graph replays of the vision tower<br>`async_tensor_h2d` for cos/sin/`cu_seqlens`/merge index<br>gather instead of boolean-mask span fill | `vllm/models/minimax_m3/common/encoder_cudagraph.py::MiniMaxM3EncoderCudaGraphMixin`<br>`vllm/v1/worker/encoder_cudagraph.py`<br>`vllm/models/deepseek_v41/common/vl_cudagraph.py`<br>`vllm/models/deepseek_v4/common/vision.py::build_packed_vit_metadata` | `compilation_config.cudagraph_mm_encoder` defaults to False. MiniMax-M3 images are captured (video stays eager; FP8 encoder attention needs static scales). DSV4.1 ViT replay metadata no longer syncs, through pinned async H2D and an index gather built from host-side `types`.<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#58673 merged 2026-09-28 (`55de40a2fc`). vllm-project/vllm#58499 merged 2026-09-26 (`7d8c5fe9a9`). vllm-project/vllm#56711 merged 2026-09-28 (`522101f6ed`). | A ViT replay preceded by `cudaStreamSynchronize` or `aten::item` gaps is the DSV4.1 pre-#58499 bug. Missing vision graph replays can mean `cudagraph_mm_encoder=False` or an unsupported item type. (Overlaps the `inv-vllm` encoder-CG row.) Also check the frozen-head backend, shape, graph/capture and fallback guards before attributing the split to an old version. |
| Attention / Mamba metadata-build overhead reduction (GPU idle between steps) | `_token_request_mapping_kernel` (via `CommonAttentionMetadata.token_to_req_indices`)<br>preallocated `cu_seqlens_q_buffer`<br>GDN `update_block_table` reuse<br>skipped `compute_causal_conv1d_metadata` | `vllm/model_executor/layers/attention/sparse_mla_attention.py::SparseMLACommonMetadataBuilder`<br>`vllm/v1/attention/ops/metadata.py::_token_request_mapping_kernel`<br>`vllm/v1/attention/backends/mla/flashattn_mla_sparse.py`<br>`vllm/v1/attention/backends/gdn_attn.py`<br>`vllm/v1/attention/backends/mamba_attn.py::needs_causal_conv1d_metadata`<br>`vllm/models/qwen4_exp/nvidia/ple_layer.py` | Sparse-MLA `req_id_per_token` used numpy repeat plus a pinned H2D every step. It is now one Triton kernel over device `query_start_loc`; the PR claims 1.6–4.8x on the kernel. MRV2 builds GDN metadata once and reuses it across KV-cache groups (CUDA, plain GDN builder only). PLE short-conv skips the unused causal-conv1d metadata, and the host-side `query_start_loc_p` slicing moved to the builder.<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#58450 merged 2026-09-25 (`c88f4824ff`). vllm-project/vllm#58762 merged 2026-09-28 (`dd3bc9c72f`). vllm-project/vllm#58114 merged 2026-09-28 (`20b52e9f5b`). | Host gaps in GLM-5.3 / DSV3.2 sparse-MLA or Qwen3.8 hybrid metadata on older images are already reduced upstream. Profile `build()` time before proposing new metadata kernels. Also check the frozen-head backend, shape, graph/capture and fallback guards before attributing the split to an old version. |
| GPU→CPU sync removal (this slice's wave) | `cudaStreamSynchronize` / `aten::item` / `aten::_local_scalar_dense` gaps<br>`_clamp_work_kv_end`<br>`_pack_topk_indices`<br>`seq_lens_cpu_upper_bound` | `vllm/v1/attention/backends/mla/flashinfer_mla_sparse_sm90.py` (`_clamp_work_kv_end`, `_PLAN_SLACK`, `_pack_topk_indices`)<br>`vllm/v1/attention/backends/flashinfer.py`<br>`vllm/models/deepseek_v41/common/vl_cudagraph.py` | (1) FlashInfer SM90 sparse MLA plans from host upper bounds and reuses the plan within `_PLAN_SLACK`. A Triton kernel clamps per-work `kv_end` on device, so there is no D2H under async scheduling (#58684). With FlashInfer 0.7 the top-k indices are packed into CSR by `_pack_topk_indices` (#58069). (2) The FlashInfer metadata builder uses `seq_lens_cpu_upper_bound` when there is no spec decode, which matters for pooling (#57214). (3) DSV4.1 ViT CUDA-graph metadata (#58499, row above). (4) Mamba2 prefill APC state saves were batched with no `.tolist()` (#49371). That code was **deleted** on 2026-10-01 together with `mamba_cache_mode="all"` (#58997, `cb6458c5c`), so it is not at the frozen SHA.<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#58684 merged 2026-09-27 (`c8d7a7dd13`). vllm-project/vllm#57214 merged 2026-09-27 (`0376f81530`). vllm-project/vllm#49371 merged 2026-09-25 (`34ee85d5ca`), superseded by #58997. vllm-project/vllm#58069 merged 2026-09-23. | Use `VLLM_GPU_SYNC_CHECK=warn` to locate remaining syncs. A per-step D2H in GLM-5.3/DSV3.2 SM90 sparse-MLA decode under async scheduling can mean an image before #58684. Do not cite `mamba_mixer2.py` for #49371 at HEAD. |
| Runtime Triton JIT recompile avoidance plus startup JIT warmup / JIT monitor | multi-ms to seconds-long CPU slices in `JITFunction.run` / `triton.compile` mid-serving<br>`do_not_specialize=[...]`<br>`do_not_specialize_on_alignment`<br>`jit_monitor` warnings | `vllm/v1/worker/gpu/spec_decode/acceptance_estimator.py` (`_accumulate_kernel`, `_local_max_sumexp_kernel`, `_predict_kernel`)<br>`vllm/models/deepseek_v41/compressor.py::_ring_slot_mapping_kernel`<br>`vllm/v1/worker/gpu/input_batch.py` (dummy `idx_mapping` int32)<br>`vllm/model_executor/layers/rotary_embedding/mrope_vit_setup.py::_vit_mrope_setup_kernel`<br>`vllm/model_executor/warmup/jit_warmup_triton_helper.py`<br>`vllm/utils/jit_monitor.py` | Scalar args that vary per step (`num_reqs`, `num_tokens`, strides) are excluded from specialization. The dummy batch dtype matches real batches (int32 `idx_mapping`). Registered CUDA Triton kernels are warmed in parallel at startup (`VLLM_TRITON_JIT_WARMUP_NUM_THREADS`, default 4). The JIT monitor runs only when warmup is enabled, and `--enforce-eager` disables warmup unless fault tolerance is on.<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#57107 merged 2026-09-28 (`3d5f4d4cd5`). vllm-project/vllm#59119 merged 2026-09-29 (`30f5c01d4e`). vllm-project/vllm#58462 merged 2026-09-23 (`310f15d354`). vllm-project/vllm#58526 merged 2026-09-24. vllm-project/vllm#58582 merged 2026-09-24 (`71891bdb94`). vllm-project/vllm#58590 merged 2026-09-24 (`e30559b58f`). vllm-project/vllm#58593 merged 2026-09-24 (`074d57fce1`). | A one-off long CPU gap with Triton compile frames in a steady-state vLLM trace is a recompile. Check `jit_monitor` logs and these `do_not_specialize` lists. Under `--enforce-eager`, runtime JIT is expected, so profile after warmup. |
| XPU graphs on by default (launch-overhead reduction on Intel GPU) | XPU graph replay instead of per-kernel launches | `vllm/platforms/xpu.py`<br>`vllm/v1/worker/gpu_worker.py` (XPU graph memory estimate) | `VLLM_XPU_ENABLE_XPU_GRAPH` was removed. Graphs stay on unless sleep mode is enabled or the PyTorch version lacks support. Graph memory is now profiled on XPU too.<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#51600 merged 2026-09-24 (`dcfc17e0b1`) | XPU traces from images after #51600 should replay graphs. Eager per-kernel launches there can mean sleep mode or old PyTorch. |
| vLLM-origin MRV2 **fused multi-step draft decode**: build draft attention metadata once, then update it in place (inside the graph) for steps 2..K | `update_draft_decode_metadata`<br>`supports_draft_decode_metadata_update`<br>`get_paged_mqa_logits_metadata` refresh<br>log "Fused multi-step draft decode is not supported by attention backend(s) …" | `vllm/v1/worker/gpu/spec_decode/autoregressive/speculator.py::_configure_fused_multi_step_decode`<br>`vllm/v1/attention/backends/mla/indexer.py::update_draft_decode_metadata`<br>`vllm/v1/attention/backends/mla/flashattn_mla_sparse.py`<br>`vllm/v1/worker/gpu/spec_decode/speculator.py` | Already existed for flash_attn, flashinfer, triton_attn, triton_mla and sparse_swa. #57443 adds FA3 sparse MLA + the DSV3.2 indexer (GLM-5.3). Requires DCP=1.<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#57443 merged 2026-10-02 (`8bdd8d84`) | Per-draft-step metadata-rebuild gaps (CPU work plus H2D between draft steps) in GLM-5.3 MTP traces can mean the backend lacks this hook or DCP>1. The PR measures +13.3% throughput at concurrency 1 and +1–2% at c4/c16. |
| vLLM-origin GDN spec-decode metadata: slice trailing padded rows instead of a host-mask gather | removed `blk_table[spec_sequence_masks_cpu]` (H2D + gather kernel) | `vllm/v1/attention/backends/gdn_attn.py` | Applies to pure spec-decode batches (`num_prefills==0 and num_decodes==0`).<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#58763 merged 2026-10-01 (`87a4bf66`) | An H2D copy + index kernel in GDN metadata build for pure MTP batches is the pre-#58763 host gap. Also check the frozen-head backend, shape, graph/capture and fallback guards before attributing the split to an old version. |
| vLLM-origin XPU DeepSeek-V4 FP8 sparse decode made CUDA/XPU-graph capturable | `dequant_gather_slots` writing into a preallocated flat workspace (no per-call `torch.empty` + copy) | `vllm/models/deepseek_v4/xpu/xpu_sparse_decode_fp8.py` | XPU only.<br>Evidence: vllm-project/vllm#59159 merged 2026-10-04 (`155d23cb`) | Before #59159, DSV4 FP8 sparse decode on XPU ran eagerly with gather/copy temporaries. |
